# SageReasoning — Decision Log (active — May 2026 onwards)

Append-only record of consequential decisions. Becomes the R0 oikeiosis audit trail when operationalised in P5.

This file holds entries dated 2026-05-01 and later. Earlier entries are in the archive files listed under "Archives" below. Entry IDs are the canonical references; physical file location is operational.

## Archives

- `/operations/decision-log-archive-2026-03.md` — entries dated 2026-03-01 to 2026-03-31 (4 entries; legacy English-format dates)
- `/operations/decision-log-archive-2026-04.md` — entries dated 2026-04-01 to 2026-04-30 (~95 entries)

## Archive policy

Adopted 2026-05-04 under `D-DECISION-LOG-ARCHIVE-POLICY-ADOPTED-2026-05-04`.

- **Cadence:** quarterly. Next cuts: 2026-07-01, 2026-10-01, 2027-01-01, 2027-04-01.
- **Boundary criterion:** by date. At each quarterly cut, entries dated before the cutoff move to a new monthly-granularity archive file or files (per the Option B granularity adopted 2026-05-04).
- **Cross-reference invariant:** entry IDs remain stable across archive boundaries. The IDs are the canonical references; the physical file location is operational. To resolve an ID from elsewhere in the project, search by ID across the active log + all archive files in `/operations/`.
- **Format inheritance:** active entries follow the lean form per `/adopted/standing-protocol-cache.md` §"Lean decision-log entry". Earlier entries used the legacy format documented in their archive files.

## 2026-05-01 — D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-DRAFTS: Critical-Path Deliverables 2, 3, 8 Drafted

**Decision:** Produce the three critical-path deliverables of Phase 1 of the alt-3 retrieval-augmented mentor design as drafts under `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/`. The three are: Deliverable 2 (`canonical-framework.md` — the 9+1 mechanism taxonomy reconciling 5-mechanism / 6-mechanism / 4-stage / compact-variant shapes), Deliverable 3 (`passion-taxonomy.md` — the formal controlled vocabulary of 4 root passions, 20 sub-species, 3 eupatheiai, false-judgement template, compound-passion handling), and Deliverable 8 (`operationalised-rules.md` — the 10 operationalised scoring rules PROHAIRESIS-FILTER-001 through KATORTHOMA-PROXIMITY-001, with full Inputs / Logic / Outputs / Examples / Interpretive Moves / Cleanliness Rating per rule, plus the six-dependency map summary). The three deliverables form the critical path; the remaining 20 Phase-1 deliverables are blocked on founder approval of these three.

**Status of the deliverables:** Drafted — under founder review. Implementation status (per 0a) for each is "Designed" (file exists, structure complete, not wired or built). Decision status (per 0f) is "Under review" until founder approves; on approval, the three deliverables move from `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` to `/adopted/` as Elevated changes (per Risk Classification Record below).

**Re-derivation note (Deliverable 8):** Deliverable 8 is a **re-derivation** from the alt-3 handoff output schemas + Stoic Brain corpus (`stoic-brain-compiled.ts` + `scoring.json`) + the named worked-example anchors (philodoxia patterns / orge with children / six consecutive procedural reports / bus story / agonia in catastrophising). The architecture-exercise transcript that originally produced the operationalisations was not in front of the drafting session. The re-derivation is functionally equivalent (the live mentor was working from the same corpus and the same output schemas) but it is not transcript-faithful. The deliverable's "Honest disclosure" section names this. Founder direction at review:
- Accept the re-derivation as canonical → Deliverable 8 v1.0.0 is established.
- Mark for redo against the transcript → Deliverable 8 redrafted in a session with transcript access.

This is the load-bearing decision in the founder's review batch.

**Files produced:**
- `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/canonical-framework.md` (Deliverable 2 — 248 lines, 26.1 KB)
- `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/passion-taxonomy.md` (Deliverable 3 — 307 lines, 29.4 KB)
- `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/operationalised-rules.md` (Deliverable 8 — 635 lines, 46.0 KB)

**Reasoning:** The alt-3 architecture (D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-ADOPTED-2026-04-29) commits the deterministic engine in the middle of the translation sandwich (AC-12). The engine needs (a) a canonical taxonomy that reconciles the four divergent endpoint shapes in production today (D2), (b) a controlled vocabulary for its passion mechanisms 2, 3, 5 to consume (D3), and (c) a rule book that operationalises every mechanism with deterministic Inputs / Logic / Outputs (D8). Without these three, the remaining 20 Phase-1 deliverables — corpus inventory, index schema, retrieval interface, re-rank design, Layer-1/Layer-3 translation specifications, three-tier intake clarification, reflect endpoint design (the load-bearing AC-18 / AC-19 deliverable), score-in-reply design, progression delta design, verification design, residual seams handling, cost model, migration plan, test plan, ADR — cannot land coherently because each of them references at least one of these three foundations. The critical-path discipline matches the alt-3 handoff's "Approval Gate" specification.

**Rules served:** R0 (oikeiosis audit trail — these three deliverables are the foundation of the rule book that operationalises alt-3), 0a (status vocabulary preserved — implementation status "Designed" per the taxonomy distinction), 0b (session continuity — handoff produced; next-session prompt ready), 0c (verification framework — founder reads each deliverable directly per the verification table in the close), 0d-ii (Standard for the drafts; Elevated for eventual adoption), 0f (decision-log entry concurrent with file production), R6a (V3 methodology rules preserved — the framework is corpus-derived, not V1-replicated), R6b (unity of virtue preserved in mechanism 9 of the canonical framework and Rule 9), R6c (qualitative proximity, not numeric — preserved in mechanism 10 / Rule 10), R6d (passions diagnostic, not punitive — preserved across mechanisms 2-5 / Rules 2-5), R6e (corpus-derived, not structure-copied — explicit in all three deliverables), R7 (source fidelity — every taxonomy element traces to a Stoic Brain source), R8a/R8b/R8c/R8d (glossary tier preserved — Greek IDs in canonical fields, English-first in developer docs, English-only on user surfaces, plain English in skill contracts), AC3 (Zone 2 domains explicitly named in passion-taxonomy compliance section), R20d (relationship asymmetry preserved — mechanism 5's outputs are scoped to the practitioner; second-person passion attribution prohibited at Layer 3 per Deliverable 11 future scope), ES1 (founder-profile coverage — worked examples drawn from philodoxia, orge, agonia in catastrophising — the founder's strong-intensity passions; coverage gap for differently-passioned practitioners noted), KG3 (hub-label end-to-end contract — the canonical framework preserves the existing `mapRequestHubToContextHub` mapper unchanged; Phase 2 build will land on `/api/founder/hub` mentor branch with `hub_id: 'private-mentor'`), PR1 (single-endpoint proof discipline — the deliverables specify the engine's design; Phase-2 build proves on the reflect endpoint first per AC-19), PR5 (knowledge-gap carry-forward — four PR5 candidates flagged for future recurrence count: translation-sandwich/neuro-symbolic terminology; withholding-as-deterministic-kathekon; no-shareable-artifact constraint; build-order condition; plus a fifth candidate logged this session — re-derivation versus transcript-package distinction), PR6 (safety-critical changes — explicitly NOT engaged this session: design only, no auth/encryption/session/redirect surface touched), PR7 (decisions not made are documented — the open questions in each deliverable name what is deferred and the condition for revisit; D-A12 Graph RAG outline, D-A10 corpus expansion, score-family endpoint migration, founder-hub flow migration are all explicitly held), PR9 (stewardship findings — none surfaced this session).

**Risk classification:**
- This adoption itself: **Standard** under 0d-ii. Drafts under `/drafts/`, no live-system effect, no code touched.
- Founder approval of all three deliverables (when it occurs): **Elevated** under 0d-ii. The move from `/drafts/` to `/adopted/` adopts a governing rule book that becomes part of the canonical Stoic Brain definition. Approval is itself a separate decision-log entry.

- Phase-2 pass 2 (conversation surface build): **Critical** under PR6 + R20a perimeter.

**Impact:**

Phase-1 deliverables 4 (corpus inventory), 5 (index schema), 6 (retrieval interface), 9 (rule dependency map and engine sequencing logic), 10 (Layer 1 translation specification), 11 (Layer 3 translation specification), 13 (three-tier intake clarification specification), 14 (reflect endpoint design — including AC-18 no-shareable-artifact constraint), 15 (long-deferred questions handling), and the remainder are unblocked once the three are founder-approved.
- The next-session prompt specifies session-2 scope per the Phase-1 prompt's recommended hybrid sequencing: Deliverables 4, 9, 10, 11, 13, 14, 15.
- Two named open questions surface for founder review: (1) acceptance of Deliverable 8's re-derivation status as canonical (vs redo against transcript); (2) whether the live mentor remains the rule-operationalisation tool for refinement of these rules in future sessions.

**Status:** Drafted — under founder review. ## 2026-05-01 — D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-AUDIT: R20a Perimeter Workflow Audit Drafted (Deliverable 24)

**Decision:** Produce Deliverable 24 — Consumer Workflow Audit (R20a Perimeter) — as a draft under `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/consumer-workflow-audit.md`. The audit walks each of the eight R20a perimeter routes named in AC5 (`/api/score`, `/api/score-decision`, `/api/score-document`, `/api/score-scenario`, `/api/score-social`, `/api/reason`, `/api/reflect`, `/api/mentor/private/reflect`) end-to-end at the workflow level — server-side workflow, page-side workflow, flow distinctions, AC-18 / AC-13 / AC-17 shape, Phase-3+ migration projection, and as-built rollback baseline pointer per route. The audit was conducted at the founder's direction following the AC-18 scoping question that surfaced in the 2026-05-01 evening-reflection walkthrough and was adopted as path (b) in the predecessor session's addendum: a dedicated audit session before Phase-1 session 2, scoped to the R20a perimeter (the safety-critical scope), at the depth of the evening-reflection walkthrough.

**Status of the deliverable:** Drafted — under founder review. Implementation status (per 0a) is "Designed" (file exists, structure complete, not wired or built). Decision status (per 0f) is "Under review" until founder approves; on approval, the audit's findings inform Phase-1 session 2 scope refinements (D11, D13, D14a, D14b) and may be appended to or kept under `/drafts/` as a reference deliverable.

**Routes covered (all eight per AC5):**
1. `/api/score` — single-action scorer (page-side: `/score/page.tsx`).
2. `/api/score-decision` — multi-option decision comparator (page-side: `/ops-hub/page.tsx`; malformed-body finding surfaced).
3. `/api/score-document` — long-form document evaluator (page-side: `/score-document/page.tsx`, `/score-policy/page.tsx`; bypasses shared engine).
4. `/api/score-scenario` — ethical-scenario reasoning practice (page-side: `/scenarios/page.tsx`).
5. `/api/score-social` — pre-publish filter for social posts (page-side: `/score-social/page.tsx`).
6. `/api/reason` — universal reasoning layer (multiple callers including `/private-mentor`, `/mentor-hub`, `/ops-hub`; dual-auth pattern; engine entry point).
7. `/api/reflect` — public daily-reflection endpoint (API-only, no page-side caller; profile-update feedback loop).
8. `/api/mentor/private/reflect` — founder-only reflect endpoint (page-side: `/private-mentor/page.tsx` morning + evening rituals; future deferral-resolution surface per AC-15).

**Headline findings:**
- **No further Option-1-shaped flow ambiguities discovered.** The eight perimeter routes were checked against the architectural argument behind AC-18 (philodoxia-as-reputation-mechanism — sharable artefact at the surface where the practitioner's reasoning about themselves is at stake re-introduces the reputation mechanism the architecture was designed to remove). The argument applies specifically to the deferral-resolution surface; no other route in the perimeter triggers the same architectural concern. **Option 1 (the AC-18 scoping correction adopted 2026-05-01) is sufficient as written.**
- **Two snapshots recommended before Phase-1 session 2 begins:** `/api/reason` (engine entry point, dual-auth pattern, three-depth surface) and `/api/mentor/private/reflect` (load-bearing for D14a engine substitution and D14b deferral-resolution build). The remaining six snapshots can land alongside Phase 3+ migration planning.
- **Phase-1 session 2 scope refinements (no new deliverables required):** D11 (Layer 3 specification) gains per-consumer projection rules including reader_triggered_passions invitation-language framing for `/api/score-social` (R20d alignment), institutional-distance soft clarification for `/api/score-document` policy mode, AC-17-flag projection rules per surface; D13 (three-tier intake clarification) expands trigger catalogue to cover surface-specific codes (`OPTION_SCOPE_INCONSISTENCY`, `DOCUMENT_OBJECT_AMBIGUITY`, `RESPONSE_AMBIGUITY`, etc.); D14a + D14b proceed as scoped; D2 receives a coverage-gap amendment (five small additions for `prior_feedback`, decision-comparison aggregate projections, policy-mode shapes, quick-depth projection, dual-applicability of Table 4a).
- **Seven current-state findings independent of Phase 1** surfaced for founder triage: Ops Hub malformed body to `/api/score-decision`; missing distress handling on Ops Hub callers of `/api/score-decision` and `/api/reason`; KG1 rule 2 candidate violations (fire-and-forget inserts) on Routes 2, 3, 7's analytics inserts; fire-and-forget on safety-relevant distress-event log at `/api/reflect`; `user_id` vs `auth.user.id` discrimination at `/api/reflect` (R17 finding); partial R20a input coverage on Routes 1, 2, 6 (distress check on primary input only).

**Rules served:** R0 (oikeiosis audit trail — the audit itself is part of the trail of how alt-3 is being adopted with evidence rather than projection), R20 (vulnerable user safeguards — the perimeter is the R20a perimeter; the audit verifies its coverage), R17 (intimate data protections — the `user_id` vs `auth.user.id` finding at `/api/reflect` is an R17 finding surfaced for founder triage), 0a (status vocabulary preserved — implementation status "Designed" per the taxonomy distinction), 0b (session continuity — handoff produced; next-session prompt for Phase-1 session 2 may need updating with audit findings), 0c (verification framework — founder reads the audit deliverable directly; per-route findings are workflow-level so founder-readable without code), 0d-ii (Standard for the audit itself; Elevated for any consequent design changes adopted in Phase 1; Critical for the eventual Phase-2 builds), 0f (decision-log entry concurrent with audit completion), R5 (cost-as-health-metric awareness — the audit observed analytics-insert fire-and-forget patterns which carry both KG1 rule 2 risk and cost-tracking implications), KG3 (hub-label end-to-end contract — the audit confirms `mapRequestHubToContextHub` consistency across the perimeter), KG1 (Vercel five-rule constraint — multiple rule-2 candidate violations surfaced in current-state findings), KG2 (Haiku reliability boundary — flagged on `/api/reason` quick-depth as a constraint), KG4 (Layer 2 applicability vs wiring — `/api/reason`'s dual-auth pattern is the canonical KG4 example), PR1 (single-endpoint discipline preserved — D14b reflect-endpoint-first build order remains binding; this audit does not propose any change to it), PR5 (knowledge-gap carry-forward — the audit cited but did not re-explain prior PR5 candidates; no new candidate logged this session beyond the per-route findings), PR6 (safety-critical changes — the audit observes the perimeter; any consequent change to the distress classifier or its wrappers is automatically Critical regardless of apparent scope), PR7 (decisions not made are documented — every current-state finding is surfaced for founder triage rather than fixed in this audit; deferred decisions are explicit), PR8 (tacit-knowledge promotion — none promoted this session; observations logged), PR9 (stewardship findings — the seven current-state findings are split across Catastrophic / Long-term regression / Efficiency tiers per PR9; the safety-relevant fire-and-forget at `/api/reflect` is upper Long-term regression, the others are Efficiency-tier).

**Risk classification:**
- This adoption itself: **Standard** under 0d-ii. Drafts under `/drafts/`, no live-system effect, no code touched, no manifest edit.
- Any consequent design changes to D14a / D14b / D11 / D13 in Phase-1 session 2 (when adopted): **Elevated** under 0d-ii. Becomes part of the canonical Phase-1 design.
- Phase-2 pass 1 (reflect endpoint build, AC-19, future session): **Critical** under PR6 (touches authentication / session management surface).
- Phase-2 pass 2 (conversation surface build, future session): **Critical** under PR6 + R20a perimeter.
- Triage of the seven current-state findings (when undertaken): mixed — items 4, 5 (KG1 rule 2 candidates including the safety-relevant distress-event log) are Standard-to-Elevated; item 6 (`user_id` vs `auth.user.id`) is Critical under PR6 (R17 perimeter); items 1, 2, 3 (Ops Hub page-side defects) are Standard; item 7 (partial R20a input coverage) is Elevated and warrants a perimeter-wide policy decision before any per-route fix.

**Files produced:**
- `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/consumer-workflow-audit.md` (Deliverable 24 — 1081 lines, ~18,481 words).

**Impact:**

## 2026-05-02 — D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-VALIDATED: Independent Validation of Alt-3 Architecture; Three Adjustments + Description Correction + Scope Limitation Recorded

**Decision:** Record the independent validation of the alt-3 architecture (`D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-ADOPTED-2026-04-29`) by the live private mentor. Validation verdict: **commit** the architecture, with three specific adjustments to be incorporated into the candidate rule book and one description correction to be reflected in the architecture's standing language. The validation also surfaced one scope limitation that the architecture documentation should name explicitly.

**Three adjustments (to be applied to Phase-1 Deliverable 8 — `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/operationalised-rules.md` — at packaging):**

1. **Rule 9 (VIRTUE-DOMAIN-ENGAGED-001) — Unity-thesis flag-not-reclassify for progressors.** For progressors (the practitioner population most likely to use the product), `UNITY_INCONSISTENCY` is treated as a diagnostic signal, not a hard reclassification rule. The architecture must distinguish *unstable phronesis* (genuine but not yet stable enough to reliably inform the other virtues — the typical progressor case) from *false phronesis* (misidentification of the genuine good — a different and more serious failure). Conflating the two reduces accuracy of the composite proximity score for the practitioner population the product is designed for. Rule 9 retains the unity check; the change is in how `UNITY_INCONSISTENCY` is interpreted in subsequent processing — as a per-instance diagnostic flag rather than a forced weakest-link aggregation override.

2. **Rule 8 (VALUE-INDIFFERENT-001) — Compound severity for INFLATION/DEFLATION same-root errors.** The compound INFLATION/DEFLATION error — craving recognition (over-valuing a preferred indifferent — recognition) and fearing humiliation (over-valuing the absence of a dispreferred indifferent — humiliation) as two expressions of the same false root judgement — should carry a higher severity than either error alone. Add a compound severity level to Rule 8's value-error vocabulary. This is the primary value-error pattern for the philodoxia profile and for any practitioner whose dominant value-distortion is reputation-shaped.

3. **Rule 7 (OIKEIOSIS-OBLIGATION-001) — Explicit operative-circle dependency on Rule 6.** Specify that Rule 7 uses the operative circle from Rule 6 (the actual circle from which the action operates), not the stated circle (the circle the practitioner names when describing the action). The seam between Rule 6's `STATED_OPERATIVE_CONFLICT` detection and Rule 9's dikaiosyne classification (which depends on accurate obligation status) is closed by making the dependency explicit in Rule 7's logic.

**Description correction (architectural language):** The alt-3 architecture should be described accurately as *"a deterministic engine for the rule-like components of Stoic reasoning, with honest soft-gating for the components that are not rule-like"* — not as a *"fully deterministic system"*. The OPEN_DEFERRAL mechanism (AC-14) is honest precisely because it acknowledges that the deterministic frame does not reach the interpretive core. Replacing "fully deterministic" with the corrected formulation in the architecture's standing language preserves R19's honest-positioning principle.

**Scope limitation:** The 10 rules in the candidate rule book have been calibrated against one practitioner's profile (philodoxia primary; the founder's profile per ES1). Severity weightings, prior probabilities, and compound-passion thresholds reflect that calibration. Other primary passions (philoplousia-strong, agonia-strong, penthos-strong) will require recalibration before the rule book can be applied across the full coverage envelope. This is not a design flaw — it is a scope limitation that the architecture documentation should name explicitly. Coverage expansion is a parallel track; the current rule book is sufficient for Phase 1 and Phase 2 against the founder's profile, which is the safety-critical first-user case per ES1.

**Reasoning:** The original alt-3 commit (`D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-ADOPTED-2026-04-29`) was driven by the architecture exercise in which the live private mentor produced full structured operationalisations for all ten Stoic Brain scoring rules. Subsequent independent validation by the live private mentor — examining the operationalisations as a body, not rule by rule — produced the verdict above. The three adjustments resolve specific seams the rule-by-rule operationalisation did not surface: Rule 9's unity-thesis treatment was implicitly weakest-link aggregation, which is too aggressive for progressors; Rule 8's value-error vocabulary did not name the compound case that is the primary error pattern for the practitioner population; Rule 7's circle-dependency was implicit and would silently use whichever circle the upstream pass produced first. The description correction is a precision-of-language fix consistent with R19. The scope limitation is honest acknowledgment of ES1's coverage assumption made explicit at the architectural level.

**Files this entry triggers edits to:**

- `/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-04-29e-private-mentor-rag-phase1-ALT3-close.md` — Validation Addendum section added near the top, naming the three adjustments + description correction + scope limitation as known requirements that Phase 2 implementation must incorporate.
- `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/operationalised-rules.md` (Deliverable 8) — Validation Addendum section added near the top. The rules' Logic / Outputs sections retain their drafted content; the addendum is the bridge between this entry and the rule book's existing text. A subsequent revision pass on D8 (in a future session) will fold the adjustments into the per-rule sections themselves; this session lands the addendum so the validation findings are recorded in the artefact where the rules actually live, which is what the founder review needs.

**Files explicitly NOT edited this session (with reasoning, per PR7):**

- `/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-04-29-rag-phase1-ALT3-NEXT-SESSION-PROMPT.md` — the alt-3 Phase-1 prompt. Phase-1 session 1 has already executed against this prompt (`D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-DRAFTS-2026-05-01`), making the prompt historical. Editing it now would be editing an audit-trail artefact whose purpose is already fulfilled. The validation findings are landed in the rule book itself (D8), which is the document Phase-1 session 2 (and any subsequent rule-book revision session) will read. Founder direction at session open: apply findings to D8, not to the historical prompt. Condition for revisit: not expected — once the rule book is updated, the prompt's validation-blind history is part of the audit trail and should remain as captured.

**Risk classification:** Standard under 0d-ii. Three governance documents touched (decision log append, alt-3 handoff section addition, D8 section addition). No code touched. No live system risk. Pre-edit backups for the alt-3 handoff and D8 created at session open (`/archive/2026-05-02_2026-04-29e-private-mentor-rag-phase1-ALT3-close_pre-validation-addendum.md`, `/archive/2026-05-02_operationalised-rules_pre-validation-addendum.md`). Rollback for any edit: restore from backup and append a supersession note to this entry.

**Rules served:** R0 (oikeiosis audit trail — the validation event is recorded so future sessions reading the audit trail can reconstruct the architectural reasoning, including the seam-closing adjustments that came after the initial commit), 0a (status vocabulary preserved — adjustments target an artefact in implementation-status "Designed"; this entry's decision status is "Adopted"), 0b (session continuity — the addenda in the alt-3 handoff and D8 carry forward across all subsequent sessions reading those documents), 0c (verification framework — the founder verifies the addendum text directly), 0d-ii (Standard classification justified above), 0f (decision-log entry concurrent with the documents it triggers edits to), R6b (unity of virtue — the Rule 9 adjustment refines the unity-thesis treatment to reflect the progressor case, preserving R6b's substance while distinguishing unstable from false phronesis), R6d (passions diagnostic, not punitive — the Rule 8 compound-severity adjustment refines the diagnostic vocabulary; the increased severity is a more accurate diagnostic claim, not a punitive escalation), R7 (source fidelity — the unstable-vs-false-phronesis distinction traces to Stoic source material on phronesis development, not to the practitioner's profile), R19 (honest positioning — the description correction replaces "fully deterministic" with language that honestly names what the engine does and does not deterministically resolve), ES1 (Zone 2 eval inputs include founder-profile inputs — the scope limitation makes the founder-profile calibration assumption that ES1 already names explicit at the architectural level), PR1 (single-endpoint proof discipline — these adjustments will land at the engine's Phase-2 proof endpoint per AC-19; the addenda preserve the discipline by naming what must be true at the proof endpoint before rollout), PR5 (knowledge-gap carry-forward — the three adjustments and the description correction are candidate PR5 entries on first observation; logged here for promotion on third recurrence), PR6 (safety-critical changes — explicitly NOT engaged: this is governance-document editing only; no auth / encryption / session / redirect / classifier surface touched), PR7 (decisions not made are documented — the explicit non-edit of the historical Phase-1 prompt is recorded above with reasoning and revisit condition).

**Status:** Adopted. Cross-references: D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-ADOPTED-2026-04-29 (the architecture this validates), D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-DRAFTS-2026-05-01 (the Phase-1 critical-path drafts this validation refines), D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-AUDIT-2026-05-01 (the R20a perimeter audit produced concurrently with the Phase-1 drafts — independent of this validation but contemporaneous), `/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-04-29e-private-mentor-rag-phase1-ALT3-close.md` (the alt-3 architecture handoff — Validation Addendum section added by this entry's triggered edit), `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/operationalised-rules.md` (Deliverable 8 — Validation Addendum section added by this entry's triggered edit), `/adopted/session-opening-protocol.md` (governing frame).

## 2026-05-02 — D-REGISTRY-UPDATE-v1.3.0: Component Registry Update — Alt-3 Governance Artefacts + Transitive Blocker Updates

## 2026-05-02 — D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-CRITICAL-PATH-APPROVED: Founder Approval of D2 / D3 / D8 (Path A)

**Decision:** Founder approved the three Phase-1 critical-path deliverables (D2 — canonical mechanism framework; D3 — passion taxonomy; D8 — operationalised scoring rules with Validation Addendum) as drafted. Path A under the Phase-1 session 2 prompt's pre-condition options (a / b / c / d): "Approve all three as drafted." D8 becomes v1.0.0 with the Validation Addendum carried forward to a future revision pass that produces v1.1.0. Phase-1 session 2 proceeds against the addendum-only state of D8 — the addendum's per-adjustment implementation guidance is sufficient direction for D9 / D11 / D13.

The founder also confirmed D24 (R20a consumer-workflow audit) reviewed and accepted as found; current-state findings (Ops Hub malformed body; Ops Hub missing distress handling; KG1 rule 2 candidate violations on analytics inserts; fire-and-forget on safety-relevant distress-event log at /api/reflect; user_id vs auth.user.id at /api/reflect — Critical under PR6/R17; partial R20a input coverage on Routes 1, 2, 6) are logged for separate triage outside the Phase-1 design scope.

**Reasoning:** The Phase-1 session 2 prompt at `/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-05-01-rag-phase1-alt3-NEXT-SESSION-PROMPT.md` specifies that the session does not begin until both pre-conditions are met:

1. Founder approval of the three critical-path deliverables.
2. D24 (R20a perimeter workflow audit) drafted and reviewed.

Both pre-conditions are met as of this entry. The approval unblocks downstream Phase-1 deliverables (D4, D9, D10, D11, D13, D14a, D14b, D15) which are produced in this same session under D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-SESSION2-DRAFTS-2026-05-02.

The Validation Addendum's per-adjustment implementation guidance (Rule 7 explicit operative-circle dependency on Rule 6; Rule 8 compound severity for INFLATION/DEFLATION same-root errors; Rule 9 unity-thesis flag-not-reclassify for progressors) is incorporated into D9 (engine sequencing, Dependency 5b and Dependency 4 interaction; Dependency 6 conditional logic), D11 (Layer 3 Refinement 5 — Validation Addendum prose projection), and D13 (back-edge interaction notes). The transcript-faithful redo of D8 to v1.1.0 happens in a future session when the architecture-exercise transcript is surfaced.

**File-move-to-`/adopted/` housekeeping (separate Elevated-risk action):** D2, D3, D8 currently sit under `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/`. The approval-gate footer of each deliverable specifies the move to `/adopted/` as a separate Elevated-risk action requiring its own decision-log entry. **The move is not executed in this session;** it is surfaced to the founder for separate scheduling. Until the move is executed, the deliverables retain `Designed` implementation status under the 0a vocabulary (drafted under founder review = Designed; approved-as-drafted = Designed → moves to Verified once the file-move + decision-log entry close out the gate).

**Rules served:** R0 (oikeiosis — the audit trail entry for the approval); 0a (implementation status vocabulary preserved — Designed remains the status until the move completes); 0c (verification framework — founder verified by AskUserQuestion at session open; founder reads the deliverables directly); 0f (decision-log entry concurrent with the approval); PR7 (decisions not made are documented — the file move to `/adopted/` is explicitly deferred with revisit condition: founder schedules the move as a separate Elevated-risk action).

**Status:** Adopted. ## 2026-05-02 — D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-SESSION2-DRAFTS: Phase-1 Session 2 Deliverables (D4, D9, D10, D11, D13, D14a, D14b, D15) Drafted

**Decision:** Eight Phase-1 alt-3 deliverables produced as drafts under `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/`:

- **D4 — Corpus inventory** → `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/corpus-inventory.md`. Tagging schema (passage_id, source_file, source_citation, passage_type, canonical_mechanism, passion, sub_passion, audience_tier); per-file structural inventory across the 8 source files; coverage gaps surfaced (D-A16 focus-question stems; D-A10 corpus expansion limits).
- **D9 — Rule dependency map and engine sequencing logic** → `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/rule-dependency-map.md`. Six dependencies in full treatment; canonical engine sequencing 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5(p) → 6 → 7(p) → 8 → 9 → 5(e) → 7(c) → 10; conditional back-edge from Rule 8 to Rules 2/3 with `value_error_without_passion_flag` trigger and 1-re-run loop guard; Validation Addendum guidance incorporated (Adjustment 1 conditional at Rule 10; Adjustment 2 compound-severity interaction with back-edge; Adjustment 3 explicit Rule 6 → Rule 7 operative-circle dependency).
- **D10 — Layer 1 translation specification** → `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/layer-1-translation.md`. Narrow-scope specification (entities, temporal/evaluative axes, scope markers, target identifiers, indifferents); controlled vocabulary from D3 + value.json; prompt template with cache discipline per AC6; ELEMENT_FUSION + empty-schema-with-reason + validation-failure error handling; worked examples for all named anchor patterns (philodoxia, orge with children, six procedural reports, bus story, agonia in catastrophising, ELEMENT_FUSION case).
- **D11 — Layer 3 translation specification** → `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/layer-3-translation.md`. Inclusion + exclusion strict prompting; per-consumer projection rules covering D2 Tables 1, 2, 4a, 4b, 5; AC-10 slot-fill mechanics for focus questions; D24 audit refinements incorporated (Refinement 1 — reader_triggered_passions invitation-language for `/api/score-social`; Refinement 2 — institutional-distance soft clarification for `/api/score-document` policy mode; Refinement 3 — AC-17 flag projection per surface; Refinement 4 — Table 4a dual applicability; Refinement 5 — Validation Addendum Adjustment 1 prose projection); R20d second-person passion attribution prohibited.
- **D13 — Three-tier intake clarification specification** → `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/three-tier-intake.md`. Tier 1 force / Tier 2 soft / Tier 3 deterministic-withhold model; engine-level vs surface-level trigger distinction; full trigger catalogue (7 engine-level + 12 surface-level codes per D24 audit findings); OPEN_DEFERRAL data structure; timestamping logic; interaction with engine sequencing per D9.
- **D14a — Daily-reflection ritual endpoint design** → `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/reflect-endpoint-14a-daily-ritual.md`. Visible-output preservation per D2 Table 4a; surface design recommendation (own page over embedded view — founder calls); morning/evening symmetry with `ritual_type` parameter recommendation; persistence pipeline preserved; pattern-engine pass per ADR-PE-01 preserved; founder-performable verification specification; Phase-2 build sequence (snapshot → engine implementation → env-flag deployment → verification → page move → mentor_observation surfacing → audit-finding integration).
- **D14b — Deferral-resolution surface design** → `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/reflect-endpoint-14b-deferral-resolution.md`. **The load-bearing Phase-2 pass-1 deliverable per AC-19.** New route recommendation (`/api/mentor/private/deferral-resolve`); new page recommendation (`/private-mentor/deferred-questions`); structured intake form shape; deferred-question presentation logic (engine-composed at scoring time, presented verbatim at resolution time); reflection content processing through Tier 1/2/3; retrospective score update mechanism with new `open_deferrals` and `deferral_resolutions` schema tables; **AC-18 architectural specification** (what surface DOES NOT and DOES produce); R17 intimate data protection conformance (RLS, application-level encryption per R17b, cascade deletion per R17c); AC5 ninth-route discipline; founder-performable verification protocol; Phase-2 pass-1 build readiness with Critical Change Protocol applied per PR6.
- **D15 — Long-deferred questions handling** → `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/long-deferred-questions.md`. Three principles operationalised: Principle 1 (engine doesn't nag — operational specification of what "nag" means); Principle 2 (OPEN_DEFERRAL flags visible in scoring record — data structure + surface presentation, visible but not celebratory); Principle 3 (mentor names pattern at next natural opportunity — domain-match algorithm across four axes; long-deferred threshold N=7 days; observation language constraints; worked example).

**Reasoning:** Per the Phase-1 session 2 prompt at `/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-05-01-rag-phase1-alt3-NEXT-SESSION-PROMPT.md`, the session's deliverables are eight design documents that follow Phase-1 session 1's three critical-path drafts. ## 2026-05-02 — D-MENTOR-PRIVATE-REFLECT-PRE-ALT3-SNAPSHOT: Approach B Documentary Snapshot Produced

**Decision:** Per D24 §"Route 8 — As-built rollback baseline pointer" recommendation and per founder direction post-session-2-close (Approach B), produce a documentary snapshot of `/api/mentor/private/reflect` end-to-end behaviour as it stands at git ref `1e7cffa`. The snapshot captures the route's full server-side workflow (rate limit → founder-only auth → body parse → R20a check → context loading → pattern-engine pass → user-message construction → direct Sonnet call with `REFLECTION_PROMPT` → response parse with graceful-degradation fallback → reflections insert → reasoning receipt → structured observation extraction and pipeline log → analytics insert → self-improving feedback loop → response envelope), the page-side `submitRitual` flow (textarea → distress branch → formatted bubble rendering with `**proximity** — sage_perspective` plus italicised `*evening_prompt*` → cleanup → proximity ring refresh), the full `REFLECTION_PROMPT` text, the `reflections` table schema reference, and the architectural facts captured for post-build comparison (visible output shape; persistence shapes; diagnostic fields; latency profile; R20a perimeter compliance). Snapshot written to `/archive/2026-05-02_api-mentor-private-reflect_pre-alt-3-snapshot.md`.

**Reasoning:** The D24 audit identified two snapshots before Phase-2 begins as the only urgency recommendation (`/api/reason` and `/api/mentor/private/reflect`). The founder direction at session-2 close was to defer in favour of D14a's env-flag-gated dual-path verification (Approach C). Post-session-2-close, the founder revised to take both Approach B (snapshot) **and** Approach C (env flag from D14a). The snapshot is now produced as a documentary record; the env-flag mechanism remains the comparison protocol per D14a §"Verification 1 — Visible output preserved."

The snapshot serves three concrete purposes:

1. **Phase-2 pass-2 verification reference.** When Phase-2 pass 2 substitutes the deterministic engine for the direct Claude call, the snapshot is the comparison reference. With `MENTOR_RAG_V1=false`, observed behaviour should match the snapshot. With `MENTOR_RAG_V1=true`, observed behaviour should produce semantically equivalent visible-output content.
2. **Rollback verification reference.** If Phase-2 pass 2 surfaces issues and the founder rolls back via the env flag, the snapshot is what the rollback target looks like. The snapshot is the operational definition of "today's behaviour preserved."
3. **AC4 invocation testing reference.** The snapshot names `enforceDistressCheck(detectDistressTwoStage(combinedInput))` as the canonical R20a call pattern on this route. The Phase-2 build's invocation tests verify the call appears in the execution path post-substitution.

The snapshot is not a runnable duplicate; it is documentary. Approach A (baseline duplicate route + page) was not chosen — the founder's call is that env-flag verification (sequential comparison) is sufficient for the Phase-2 verification need.

**`/api/reason` snapshot deferred.** D24 also recommended a `/api/reason` snapshot before Phase-1 session 2; it remains unproduced. Phase-2 pass-3 build (conversation surface migration) is downstream of Phase-2 passes 1 and 2; the `/api/reason` snapshot can land alongside Phase-2 pass-3 planning without blocking Phase-1 session 3 design or Phase-2 pass-1 / pass-2 builds. Logged for the founder's awareness; not blocking.

**Files touched:**
- `/archive/2026-05-02_api-mentor-private-reflect_pre-alt-3-snapshot.md` — new (documentary).

## 2026-05-02 — D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-CRITICAL-PATH-MOVED-TO-ADOPTED: D2 / D3 / D8 Moved from `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` to `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/`

**Decision:** Move the three Phase-1 critical-path deliverables (D2 — canonical mechanism framework; D3 — passion taxonomy; D8 — operationalised scoring rules with Validation Addendum, v1.0.0) from `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` to `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/`. Three `git mv` operations preserve git history. Plus three header updates and three cross-reference path updates within the moved files (D3's reference to D2; D8's references to D2 and D3). D2 contains no `/drafts/`-pathed cross-references and required only a header update. The destination subfolder `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/` mirrors the source structure (`/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/`) so the alt-3 deliverables remain grouped within `/adopted/` as the rest of Phase 1's deliverables eventually move there.

**Reasoning:** Per the deliverables' own approval-gate footers (e.g., D8 footer: *"When approved, this rule book moves from `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` to `/adopted/`. The move is itself a separate decision-log entry."*), the move is a separate Elevated-risk action distinct from the approval itself. The approval landed in D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-CRITICAL-PATH-APPROVED-2026-05-02 (Path A, founder approved as drafted). The move is now executed under this entry.

The destination convention (`/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/` subfolder rather than flat `/adopted/`) is the minimum-surprise read of the deliverables' approval-gate footer language ("moves from `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` to `/adopted/`" preserved as group). The existing flat `/adopted/` (canonical-patterns.md, canonical-sources.md, session-opening-protocol.md) holds top-level governance docs; the alt-3 deliverables are project-grouped artefacts that mirror their source grouping. As Phase-1 session 3 deliverables and the eventual ADR (D1) move to `/adopted/`, they will land at `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/<deliverable>.md` to preserve the grouping.

**File moves (via `git mv`):**
- `drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/canonical-framework.md` → `adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/canonical-framework.md` (D2).
- `drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/passion-taxonomy.md` → `adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/passion-taxonomy.md` (D3).
- `drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/operationalised-rules.md` → `adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/operationalised-rules.md` (D8 v1.0.0 with Validation Addendum carried forward).

**Header updates within moved files:**
- D2: Status line updated from "Drafted (under founder review)" to "Adopted (founder approval per Path A on 2026-05-02; D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-CRITICAL-PATH-APPROVED-2026-05-02). Moved from `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` to `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/` 2026-05-02." Critical-path language amended from "must be approved before downstream Phase-1 deliverables proceed" to "form the critical path" (the approval has happened; the descriptive language reflects the present state).
- D3: same pattern of Status line + Critical-path language updates.
- D8: Status line updated to "Adopted as v1.0.0" (preserves the v1.0.0 versioning per the Validation Addendum revision-pass commitment); Critical-path language amended.

**Cross-reference path updates within moved files:**
- D3's cross-reference to D2 updated from `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/canonical-framework.md` to `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/canonical-framework.md`.
- D8's cross-references to D2 and D3 updated similarly.
- D2 contains no `/drafts/`-pathed cross-references to D3 / D8 directly (it references the alt-3 handoff and the manifest by absolute paths; D3 / D8 are referenced as "Deliverable 3" / "Deliverable 8" in body prose, which need no path update).

**Cross-references in other documents — known-stale and intentionally not updated this session:**

The eight Phase-1 session-2 deliverables (D4, D9, D10, D11, D13, D14a, D14b, D15) reference D2 / D3 / D8 at `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` paths in their cross-references blocks. These references will become stale post-this-move. **Per fast-bounded session principle, these references are not updated in this housekeeping action.** They will update when the session-2 deliverables themselves move to `/adopted/` (likely as part of Phase-1 session 3's eventual approval batch when all 23 deliverables move together; or as a focused housekeeping action between sessions).

The session-2 close (`/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-05-02-rag-phase1-alt3-session2-close.md`) and the session-3 next-session prompt (`/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-05-02-rag-phase1-alt3-session3-NEXT-SESSION-PROMPT.md`) also reference D2 / D3 / D8 at `/drafts/` paths. The session-3 prompt's read sequence in Part A is conditional ("Read in full (or their `/adopted/`-version if option (a) of the preconditions was taken)") — so the prompt language anticipates the move and resolves correctly post-move.

The decision log itself (this entry's predecessor entries — D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-VALIDATED, D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-DRAFTS, etc.) references D2 / D3 / D8 at `/drafts/` paths. These are historical entries; per the append-only discipline (project instructions 0f), historical entries are not retroactively edited. The decision log accepts that historical entries reference predecessor file locations.

The component registry (`/website/public/component-registry.json`) tracks D2, D3, D8, D24 as registry entries. The registry's `path` fields reference `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` paths. Per the registry-update skill discipline, a registry update (v1.3.1 or v1.4.0) is the appropriate vehicle to reflect the moves to `/adopted/`. **This is logged as a follow-up housekeeping item.** Phase-1 session 3 close or a separate registry-update session resolves.

**Risk classification:** Elevated under 0d-ii. The move is a structural change to governing-document locations. Specifically:

- **What could break:** cross-references in other documents (the session-2 deliverables, the session-2 close, the session-3 prompt, the decision-log historical entries, the component registry) point to the old paths and become stale. Practitioners reading those documents and clicking through to D2 / D3 / D8 at the old paths will get file-not-found errors.
- **What does not break:** the live website (no website file references D2 / D3 / D8); the engine implementation (Phase 2 hasn't built yet); auth / session / cookie behaviour (not touched); the database (not touched).
- **Rollback path:** `git mv adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/<file> drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/<file>` (three reverse moves). Plus revert the header / cross-reference edits via git's revert mechanism on this commit. Reversible at any time before the next dependent action.
- **Verification step (founder-performable):** `ls adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/` should show three files (canonical-framework.md, passion-taxonomy.md, operationalised-rules.md). `ls drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` should show the eight session-2 deliverables plus consumer-workflow-audit.md (D24) — D2 / D3 / D8 absent. Each moved file's first 10 lines should show "Status: Adopted" rather than "Status: Drafted."

**Stale-reference cleanup follow-ups (logged for transparency):**
1. Update component registry `path` fields for D2 / D3 / D8 / D24 entries (registry update v1.3.1 or v1.4.0 — follows the standard registry-update skill).
2. Update cross-references in the eight session-2 deliverables when they themselves move to `/adopted/` (likely in Phase-1 session 3's eventual approval batch).
3. Update cross-references in the session-2 close and session-3 prompt — these are historical handoff documents and per protocol convention are not retroactively edited; readers resolve the path mentally or via filesystem search.

- `operations/decision-log.md` — this entry appended (and D-MENTOR-PRIVATE-REFLECT-PRE-ALT3-SNAPSHOT appended above).

**Rules served:** R0 (oikeiosis audit trail — the move is part of the audit trail; D2 / D3 / D8 transition from `Drafted` to `Adopted` is recorded here); 0a (status vocabulary — the moved files now carry implementation status `Adopted`; the `Adopted` decision status of the approval is preserved in D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-CRITICAL-PATH-APPROVED-2026-05-02; the two taxonomies remain separate per the 0a discipline); 0c (verification framework — founder-performable verification step named above); 0d-ii (Elevated classification justified above; rollback path documented; founder approval covered by the predecessor approval entry plus this move's classification); 0f (decision-log entry concurrent with the move); PR1 (single-endpoint proof discipline preserved — D2 / D3 / D8 are governance documents, not endpoints; the move is internal to the documentation hierarchy and does not affect the alt-3 architecture's single-endpoint commitment); PR7 (decisions not made are documented — stale-reference cleanup deferred with named follow-ups).

## 2026-05-02 — D-A16-CATALOGUE-ASSEMBLED-AND-ADOPTED: Focus-Question-Stem Catalogue Assembled and Adopted

**Decision:** Assemble and adopt the focus-question-stem catalogue (D-A16) as a new alt-3 deliverable. The catalogue specifies 27 stem entries across 21 distinct trigger codes (19 from D13's catalogue + 2 ritual codes introduced by this catalogue) with full slot_fields metadata, source citations, trigger_condition / intake_tier tagging, and the three catalogue analytics flags (eupatheia_boundary_relevant, praxis_motivation_relevant, validation_addendum_aware). The catalogue file is `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/d-a16-catalogue.md`. Phase-2 pass-1 blocking minimum (T3-001 EUPATHEIA_BOUNDARY + T3-002 PRAXIS_MOTIVATION_AMBIGUITY) is met with full slot_fields specifications.

**Reasoning:** Per the session prompt at `/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-05-02-d-a16-catalogue-assembly-NEXT-SESSION-PROMPT.md`, the catalogue is a Phase-2 pass-1 precondition per D21 §"Pre-build prerequisites" + D14b §"Phase-2 pass 1 build readiness". Without the catalogue's EUPATHEIA_BOUNDARY + PRAXIS_MOTIVATION_AMBIGUITY stems, Phase-2 pass 1 cannot deploy. Without the additional Tier 1 / Tier 2 / surface-specific / ritual coverage, Phase-2 passes 2+ would have catalogue gaps at their respective build times.

The session executed the prompt's four work streams in revised form per founder direction at session open (Path A — Use D13 + alt-3 handoff as the source set; the prompt's premise that mentor-knowledge-base.ts and REFLECTION_PROMPT contain canonical stems was wrong — Findings 1-2 below). Stream 1 (source extraction) revised to extract from D13 (the de facto stem source, already structured) and the alt-3 handoff lines 122-124, 127, 130-133 (one alternative ELEMENT_FUSION formulation). Stream 2 (decompose into stems) executed against D13's stem text with explicit slot_fields metadata structuring. Stream 3 (tagging + coverage check) confirmed 21 distinct trigger codes covered (all 7 engine-level + 12 surface-level from D13 + 2 ritual codes). Stream 4 (founder review) surfaced two direction calls captured in §"Founder direction — resolved 2026-05-02" of the catalogue document.

**Findings surfaced at session open (recorded for the audit trail):**

1. **`mentor-knowledge-base.ts` is not a question-pattern catalogue.** The session prompt described it as "the existing question-pattern catalogue used by the live mentor" containing question patterns to extract. The actual file (103 lines, located at `website/src/data/mentor-knowledge-base.ts` — not `website/src/lib/sage-mentor/...` as the prompt cited) contains two informational documents only: STOIC_HISTORICAL_CONTEXT (historical evolution of Stoicism + knowledge-arbitrage concept) and GLOBAL_STATE_OF_HUMANITY (population, technology, ecology summary). Zero question patterns. It is a Layer 5 background briefing per its own header comment (`Layer 5: Mentor Knowledge Base v1.0.0 (2026-04-10)`), not a question catalogue.

2. **`REFLECTION_PROMPT` is a meta-prompt, not a stem catalogue.** The session prompt described it as carrying "every question pattern within the template" with embedded sample questions. The actual REFLECTION_PROMPT (lines 81-125 of `route.ts` at git ref `1e7cffa`) is a 4-stage evaluation instruction to Claude with a JSON schema. The `evening_prompt` field's spec reads as a schema instruction telling Claude to compose the question live on each call (`<1 sentence: a reflective question for the user to sit with tonight, drawn from their specific situation>`). No canonical stems exist in the prompt itself.

3. **The actual stem catalogue is substantially in D13 already.** D13 (three-tier intake) carries 17 stems with worked examples and slot_fields metadata across the canonical engine-level catalogue (7 stems: 3 Tier 1, 2 Tier 2, 2 Tier 3) plus surface-level catalogue (12 stems across 5 consumer routes). The two Tier 3 stems that pass-1 blocking requires (EUPATHEIA_BOUNDARY + PRAXIS_MOTIVATION_AMBIGUITY) are already fully specified in D13 §"Engine-level Tier 3 triggers (full text)" with stems and slot_fields.

The founder's direction at session open (Path A — Use D13 + alt-3 handoff) authorised the revised source set. The session proceeded with the catalogue assembly task structurally intact — the stems get assembled, decomposed, tagged, and surfaced for review — but with the source materials shifted to what actually exists.

**Founder direction calls (resolved at this session):**

1. **Approval pathway — Path B equivalent ("Don't defer things, do them first") — confirmed.** The catalogue is accepted as drafted with the philodoxia-variant addition AND moved to `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/` in the same session, rather than staying in `/drafts/` for a subsequent promotion session. Same operational signal as Path B in the Phase-1 completion review (D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-COMPLETION-REVIEW-APPROVED-2026-05-02) — bias toward execution rather than deferral.

2. **RIT-E-001b philodoxia variant — Yes, add this session — confirmed.** A passion-specific evening flattering-story stem variant added to the catalogue, tagged `passion: epithumia` / `sub_passion: philodoxia`. Phase-2 build's Layer 3 prompt selects between RIT-E-001 (canonical) and RIT-E-001b (philodoxia-detected) per the selection rule documented in RIT-E-001b's notes. Honours ES1 (founder profile).

**Catalogue contents (27 entries across 21 distinct trigger codes):**

- **Tier 3 engine-level (Phase-2 pass-1 blocking minimum):** T3-001 EUPATHEIA_BOUNDARY + T3-002 PRAXIS_MOTIVATION_AMBIGUITY.
- **Tier 1 engine-level:** T1E-001 (canonical) + T1E-002 (alt-3 handoff variant) for ELEMENT_FUSION; T1E-003 SCOPE_AMBIGUITY; T1E-004 TEMPORAL_AMBIGUITY.
- **Tier 2 engine-level:** T2E-001 STATED_OPERATIVE_CONFLICT; T2E-002 STATED_EQUANIMITY_UNVERIFIED.
- **Tier 1 surface-level (per D24):** T1S-001 OPTION_SCOPE_INCONSISTENCY; T1S-002 OPTION_FALSE_ALTERNATIVE; T1S-003 DOCUMENT_OBJECT_AMBIGUITY; T1S-004 DOCUMENT_PURPOSE_AMBIGUITY; T1S-005 RESPONSE_AMBIGUITY; T1S-006 POST_ELEMENT_FUSION; T1S-007 REFLECTION_NARRATIVE_THIN; T1S-008 RESPONSE_FIELD_INCONSISTENCY.
- **Tier 2 surface-level (per D24):** T2S-001 STATED_PROCESS_INCONSISTENCY; T2S-002 POLICY_INSTITUTIONAL_DISTANCE; T2S-003 RESPONSE_SCENARIO_DRIFT; T2S-004 POST_PURPOSE_AMBIGUITY.
- **Ritual surface (D14a — alt-3 derived this session):** RIT-M-001 morning-indifferent-awareness; RIT-M-002 morning-circle-and-virtue; RIT-M-003 morning-passion-watchpoint; RIT-E-001 evening-flattering-story-canonical; **RIT-E-001b evening-flattering-story-philodoxia (founder direction)**; RIT-E-002 evening-false-judgement-then-and-now; RIT-E-003 evening-virtue-deficiency-pattern.

**Validation Addendum awareness flags (3 stems):** T3-002 PRAXIS_MOTIVATION_AMBIGUITY (Adjustment 1 — unstable vs false phronesis); T2E-001 STATED_OPERATIVE_CONFLICT (Adjustment 3 — operative-circle dependency); RIT-E-003 evening-virtue-deficiency-pattern (Adjustment 1 — unity check interpretation). The flag is for catalogue analytics; the actual Validation-Addendum-aware prose lives in Layer 3's Refinement 5 projection (per D11) at engine output time.

**Schema seam — ritual stems and D5 corpus_passages constraint.** D5's CHECK constraint requires `intake_tier ∈ {1, 2, 3}` for `passage_type: focus_question_stem`. The 7 ritual stems are not intake-clarification stems (they fire as Layer 3 projection on every successful ritual evaluation, not as a clarification trigger). Three resolution paths named in the catalogue's §"Schema seam — ritual stems and D5 corpus_passages constraint" section: (A) D5 schema amendment — Elevated, requires re-approval; (B) synthetic intake_tier: 1 assignment with trigger_condition carrying the semantic distinction — Standard, the catalogue's default; (C) defer to Phase-2 build operational decisions. The catalogue uses Path B as documentation default; Phase-2 build can switch to Path A at its own time.

**Files touched (this session — design-only, no live-system effect):**

- `adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/d-a16-catalogue.md` — new file, written this session and moved to `/adopted/`. ~750 lines. Status: Adopted.
- `operations/decision-log.md` — this entry appended.
- `operations/handoffs/founder/2026-05-02-d-a16-catalogue-assembly-close.md` — new (the session close, produced concurrently with this entry).

**Pre-edit backups:** none required (new files; no overwrites of existing content).

**Risk classification:** Standard under 0d-ii. The catalogue is documentation; the move to `/adopted/` is a brand-new file with zero incoming cross-references (no stale references to break). No code touched; no live-system effect; no schema migrations; no auth/encryption/session/redirect surfaces engaged. AC7 not engaged. PR6 not engaged. Critical Change Protocol not engaged.

The eventual Phase-2 build of the catalogue's entries into the `corpus_passages` Supabase table is a separate Critical-risk decision-log entry per D5 §"Step 2 — D-A16 catalogue promotion" + PR6. The build inserts (at minimum) T3-001 + T3-002 at Phase-2 pass 1; the additional stems land at passes 2 and 3+ per D21's migration plan.

**Rules served:** R0 (oikeiosis audit trail — the catalogue is part of the audit trail; the 27 stems honour the discipline that focus questions are application-layer constructions with explicit citations); 0a (status vocabulary — implementation status `Adopted` for the catalogue file; this entry's decision status `Adopted` per the 0a separation); 0b (session continuity — handoff read at open; session close concurrent with this entry); 0c (verification framework — founder-performable verification specifications named in §"Founder Verification" of the session close); 0d-ii (Standard classification justified above; the brand-new-file move-to-`/adopted/` does not match the Elevated precedent of D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-CRITICAL-PATH-MOVED-TO-ADOPTED-2026-05-02, which involved 22 files with extensive cross-reference drift); 0e (file organisation — `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/` mirrors the alt-3 grouping per the prior precedent); 0f (decision-log entry concurrent with the catalogue); PR1 (single-endpoint proof preserved — D14b is named as Phase-2 pass-1's single-endpoint target per AC-19; this catalogue's role is to provide pass-1's required content); PR4 (model selection — N/A this session; design only); PR5 (knowledge-gap carry-forward — KG7 JSONB array discipline named in slot_fields specifications; KG6 composition order applies to the corpus_passages reads at retrieval time; KG2 Sonnet boundary not engaged at session level); PR6 (safety-critical changes are Critical — Phase-2 pass 1 build remains Critical at its own time per D21); PR7 (decisions not made are documented — schema seam resolution path deferred to Phase-2 build per Path C documentation default; selection rules for stem alternatives at runtime deferred to Phase-2 build operational decisions; corpus expansion D-A10 interaction logged for awareness).

**Status:** Adopted. Cross-references: D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-COMPLETION-REVIEW-APPROVED-2026-05-02 (the Phase-1 design batch this catalogue extends; the precondition met); D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-VALIDATED-2026-04-29 (the Validation Addendum honoured by 3 catalogue entries); D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-SESSION2-DRAFTS-2026-05-02 (D11/D13/D14a/D14b drafts that name the catalogue's source content); D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-PHASE1-SESSION3-DRAFTS-2026-05-02 (D5 — the index schema this catalogue's entries materialise into); D-MENTOR-PRIVATE-REFLECT-PRE-ALT3-SNAPSHOT-2026-05-02 (the snapshot referenced by D14a's verification protocol); D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-ADOPTED-2026-04-29 (the architecture); the new catalogue file `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/d-a16-catalogue.md`; the session close.

**Reasoning:** Three governance events landed since v1.3.0 (2026-05-02 morning):

1. **D-RAG-MENTOR-ALT3-CRITICAL-PATH-MOVED-TO-ADOPTED-2026-05-02.** D2 / D3 / D8 moved from `/drafts/rag-mentor-alt3/` to `/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/`; the registry's three existing entries had stale `/drafts/` paths.

## 2026-05-02 — D-API-REASON-PRE-ALT3-SNAPSHOT: `/api/reason` Pre-Alt-3 Documentary Snapshot Produced

**Decision:** Per D24 §"Snapshots needed" + §"Route 6 — As-built rollback baseline pointer" (the audit's single highest-priority snapshot recommendation) and per the Phase-2 pass-3 readiness requirement in D21's migration plan, produce a documentary snapshot of `/api/reason` end-to-end behaviour as it stands at git ref `0820b1d` (working tree clean; preceding commit `13414ad component registry` is the v1.4.0 registry update). The snapshot captures the route's full server-side workflow (rate limit → dual-mode auth → body parse → length validation → R20a check on primary `input` field → depth validation → parallel context loading [Layer 2 practitioner gated by `auth.user?.id`; Layer 3 project at `'condensed'`] + synchronous Layer 1 Stoic Brain at depth → engine invocation via `runSageReason` → response envelope), the engine-internal workflow (depth resolution → user-message construction with optional `context`, `domain_context`, practitioner context, project context, urgency context → cache check → system-message assembly with three blocks max [depth-specific system prompt, optional Stoic Brain, optional agent brain] → Anthropic call with KG2-conformant model selection [Haiku for `quick`; Sonnet for `standard`/`deep`] → JSON parse with one retry-on-failure [`quick` escalates Haiku → Sonnet per AC1 retry budget; `standard`/`deep` retry same model] → diagnostic validation → required-field validation per depth → reasoning receipt via `extractReceipt` → cache write → result assembly with R3 disclaimer), the five distinct page-side caller flows (Caller A — `/private-mentor` proximity-ring; Caller B — `/mentor-hub` two call sites; Caller C — `/ops-hub` `handleStoicCheck` + `handleAlertEvaluation`; Caller D — `/mentor-index` skill discovery; plus Callers E/F/G/H — engine reuse, skill registry, composability hints, internal direct-function composition), the full text of all three depth-specific system prompts (`QUICK_SYSTEM_PROMPT` / `STANDARD_SYSTEM_PROMPT` / `DEEP_SYSTEM_PROMPT` — preserved per R7 source fidelity), the statelessness disclosure (no DB writes at this route — KG1 rule 2 N/A; KG3 N/A), and the architectural facts captured for post-build comparison (visible output shape per depth; D2 mapping references; diagnostic fields; latency profile; R20a perimeter compliance; dual-auth contract as canonical KG4 reference; KG2 model selection; AC-13 / AC-17 wiring as load-bearing for the entire R20a perimeter). Snapshot written to `/archive/2026-05-02_api-reason_pre-alt-3-snapshot.md` (~430 lines).

**Reasoning:** The D24 audit identified `/api/reason` as the **highest-priority snapshot in the perimeter** (D24 §"Route 6 — As-built rollback baseline pointer") because (1) it is the engine entry point and the most architecturally central of the eight perimeter routes; (2) its dual-auth pattern + three-depth shape are not captured anywhere else in the codebase; (3) Phase-2 pass 3 (conversation surface migration per D21) builds against a working engine — if `/api/reason`'s shape changes during Phase 2, downstream callers break silently. The companion snapshot for Route 8 (`/api/mentor/private/reflect`) was produced earlier today (D-MENTOR-PRIVATE-REFLECT-PRE-ALT3-SNAPSHOT-2026-05-02) and serves Phase-2 pass 2 verification; this snapshot completes the pre-alt-3 documentation set for the two snapshots D24 named urgent.

The snapshot serves three concrete purposes:

1. **Phase-2 pass-3 verification reference.** When Phase-2 pass 3 substitutes the deterministic engine for the direct Claude call inside `runSageReason`, the snapshot is the comparison reference. Pre-pass-3 behaviour reproduced (rollback path) should match the snapshot's per-depth output shapes; pass-3 engine path should produce semantically equivalent canonical fields with potentially different prose.
2. **Rollback verification reference.** If Phase-2 pass 3 surfaces issues and the founder rolls back, the snapshot is what the rollback target looks like. The snapshot is the operational definition of "today's behaviour preserved."
3. **AC4 invocation testing reference.** The snapshot names `enforceDistressCheck(detectDistressTwoStage(input))` as the canonical R20a call pattern on this route. The Phase-2 pass-3 build's invocation tests verify the call appears in the execution path post-substitution. Plus: the dual-auth contract (Step 2) and the engine's three-block system-message assembly (Engine Step 4) are documented for post-substitution structural equivalence checks.

The snapshot is not a runnable duplicate; it is documentary. Same documentary-only treatment as the companion Route 8 snapshot.

- `/archive/2026-05-02_api-reason_pre-alt-3-snapshot.md` — new (documentary).

**Pre-edit backups:** none required (new file under `/archive/`; no existing content).

## 2026-05-02 — D-D2-AMENDMENT: D2 (Canonical Mechanism Framework) Amended to v1.1.0 — Five D24 Coverage-Gap Additions

**Decision:** Amend D2 (`/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/canonical-framework.md`) with five small text additions per D24 §"Coverage gaps in D2 mapping tables" + D23 §O5.2 (logged as deferred). D2 status bumped from v1.0.0 to v1.1.0. Path A approval pathway confirmed (batch approval; bump in same session). The five amendments are all localised text additions; none changes the 9+1 mechanism set, the existing Tables 1 / 2 / 3 / 4a / 4b / 5 schemas, the cleanliness ratings, or any architectural commitment from D2 v1.0.0.

**The five amendments:**

1. **Table 1a (new) — 3-mechanism (`/api/reason` quick depth) → canonical.** Inserted immediately after Table 1 (precedent: Table 4a / 4b pattern). Captures the quick-depth shape (`control_filter`, `passion_diagnosis`, `oikeiosis`, `katorthoma_proximity`, `philosophical_reflection`, `improvement_path`, `stage_scores` 3-entry, `disclaimer`) against the canonical 9+1 set. Quick-depth uses Haiku per AC1 / KG2; the engine retry path escalates to Sonnet on parse failure (per the `/api/reason` snapshot at `/archive/2026-05-02_api-reason_pre-alt-3-snapshot.md`). The quick-depth shape is also the proximity-ring widget consumer's input shape (per D24 §"Caller A — `/private-mentor` proximity-ring widget").

2. **Coverage notes (new section) — per-route projection refinements.** Inserted between Table 1a and Table 2. Contains two named notes:
   - **Note A — `prior_feedback` (Route 1, `/api/score` iterative-refinement variant).** A Layer 1 input shape used by `/api/score`'s iterative-refinement variant; projects onto the practitioner profile's recent-interaction signals (the same surface mechanism 10's Senecan grade overlay reads from). The note recognises that *using prior evaluation findings as deliberation context for the next evaluation* is a longitudinal-reasoning feature alongside mechanism 10's profile-derived inputs. Does not introduce a new canonical mechanism.
   - **Note B — Aggregate-across-options projections (Route 2, `/api/score-decision`).** `/api/score-decision` produces aggregate projections across N independent canonical evaluations (one per decision option), distinct from the per-instance projections in Table 1. The two surface fields (`recommended` — highest-proximity option selection; `process_quality` — practitioner framing classification) live in the Layer 3 projection for the decision-comparison surface. Phase 3+ migration of `/api/score-decision` preserves this aggregate-across-options layer at the surface; the engine itself produces only per-option canonical evaluations.

3. **Table 4a heading + preamble (Routes 7 + 8 ritual flow) — dual applicability made explicit.** Heading amended from `Table 4a — REFLECTION_PROMPT daily-reflection ritual surface → canonical (visible output preserved)` to `Table 4a — REFLECTION_PROMPT daily-reflection ritual surface (Routes 7 + 8 ritual flow) → canonical (visible output preserved)`. Plus a new preamble paragraph explicitly naming both `/api/reflect` (Route 7 — public sister of the founder-only ritual surface) and `/api/mentor/private/reflect` ritual flow (Route 8 first flow — founder-only daily-reflection ritual) as Table 4a consumers. Per D24 §"Phase-1 session 2 implication": the differences between Routes 7 and 8 are upstream and downstream, not at the canonical projection.

4. **Table 6 (new) — `/api/score-document` policy-mode-specific shape → canonical.** Inserted between Table 5 and §"Cleanliness ratings per canonical mechanism". Maps three policy-mode-specific fields:
   - `deliberation_assessment` — composite read across mechanisms 7 + 9 with policy-text scoping.
   - `oikeiosis_impact` — mechanisms 6 + 7 projection scoped to affected circles (the *policy's* operative circle, not the practitioner's own).
   - `flagged_clauses[]` — per-clause structured projection (each clause is itself a mini-evaluation with its own canonical-mechanism shape).

   The policy-mode shape is the most complex of the score-family per-route projections because it introduces **per-clause nested canonical evaluations** alongside the document-level evaluation. Phase 3+ migration of `/api/score-document` policy mode requires the engine to support nested invocations.

5. **Status line update.** D2 first Status line bumped to "Adopted as v1.1.0 (founder approval per Path A on 2026-05-02; D-D2-AMENDMENT-2026-05-02 — incorporating five D24 coverage-gap additions...)" with the prior v1.0.0 Status text preserved as historical context.

File grew from 276 lines (v1.0.0) to 322 lines (v1.1.0; +46 lines). Total Table-named sections: 7 top-level + 2 sub-sections = 9 (was 5 + 2 = 7).

**Reasoning:** D24 (`/adopted/rag-mentor-alt3/consumer-workflow-audit.md`) §"Coverage gaps in D2 mapping tables" surfaced five small additions to D2's mapping tables during the D24 audit. D23 §O5.2 logged the amendments as deferred (per PR7) under the rationale that D2 was in `/adopted/` and amendment requires re-approval per D2's approval-gate footer (Elevated risk). The v1.4.0 component-registry update (D-REGISTRY-UPDATE-v1.4.0-2026-05-02) made the deferred work explicit by adding the five amendments to D2's `blocker` field as "next work."

This session executes the deferred amendment per Stream B of the prompt. The work is bounded — five localised text additions, none changing the architectural commitments. The amendments extend D2's coverage to:
- The quick-depth `/api/reason` shape (the only `/api/reason` depth not previously mapped in D2).
- Two per-route projection refinements that are upstream / aggregate concerns not in the canonical 9+1 (`prior_feedback` upstream + aggregate-across-options for decision comparison).
- The Routes 7 + 8 dual applicability of Table 4a (a clarification, not a new mapping).
- The policy-mode `/api/score-document` shape (the most complex of the score-family per-route projections; introduces per-clause nested canonical evaluations).

The 9+1 mechanism set is unchanged. The decomposition logic in Tables 1, 2, 3, 4a, 4b, 5 is unchanged. The cleanliness ratings are unchanged. R6a–R6e methodology compliance is unchanged. R7 source fidelity is unchanged.

Same operational signal as the D-A16 catalogue and the architectural-conventions catalogue (Path-B-equivalent: "do things, don't defer them"). Bias toward execution rather than deferral.

## 2026-05-02 — D-ENCRYPTION-WIRING-ADR-ADOPTED

**Decision:** Adopt ADR-ENCRYPTION-WIRING-01 (`/adopted/ADR-ENCRYPTION-WIRING-01.md`) as the architecture decision for Phase-2 pass-1's Precondition 4 (P2 task 2c — application-level encryption wiring for the new `open_deferrals` and `deferral_resolutions` tables per D14b). Five named decisions adopted: (1A) reuse existing `server-encryption.ts` module; (2A) reuse existing `MENTOR_ENCRYPTION_KEY` env var; (3A) per-column ciphertext + companion `_meta` JSONB shape matching the `mentor-profile-store.ts` precedent; (4A) founder three-copy backup ceremony (password manager + paper + Vercel env var) with monthly verification cadence; (5) env-flag-based rollback path (Path A flip `MENTOR_RAG_V1=false`; Path B `DROP TABLE`; Path C key restoration from backup). Critical Change Protocol responses pre-drafted for the eventual implementation session per R17f obligation. ADR drafted at `/drafts/`, then `mv`-moved to `/adopted/` (plain `mv`, not `git mv`, because the file was newly created and not yet under version control; git rename detection runs at commit time). Status line updated to "Adopted (founder approval per Path A on 2026-05-02; D-ENCRYPTION-WIRING-ADR-ADOPTED-2026-05-02). Moved from /drafts/ to /adopted/ 2026-05-02." Founder approved Path A (adopt + move this session) via AskUserQuestion; founder additionally approved flagging the "is `MENTOR_ENCRYPTION_KEY` already set in production with no founder backup?" question as a high-priority next-session item (per ADR §"Open questions" Q1).

**Reasoning:** The encryption-wiring ADR was the second stream of the 2026-05-02 multi-stream session (Stream 1 — Registry update v1.5.0 — landed earlier; Stream 2 — this ADR — drafted and Adopted under the same session). The ADR's purpose per Project Instructions Priority 2 §2c + D21 § Precondition 4: design the application-level encryption wiring for the two new Phase-2 pass-1 tables in advance of the Critical-risk implementation session, so the implementation session is bounded by a pre-approved architecture and can focus on execution + Critical Change Protocol + founder verification.

The ADR drafting itself is Standard risk under 0d-ii (design-only document; no code touched; no live-system effect; no auth/encryption/session/redirect surface engaged at the drafting stage). The eventual implementation session is Critical risk per R17f — that classification is preserved in the ADR's Critical Change Protocol responses (drafted in advance per R17f's obligation that "changes to encryption follow the Critical Change Protocol"; pre-drafting allows the founder to see the protocol responses in this session's context rather than discovering them at deploy time).

The five decisions converge on a "reuse the proven pattern + own the backup discipline" architecture. Reasoning per decision:

- **Decision 1 (Option 1A — reuse `server-encryption.ts`):** the module exists, is wired into `mentor-profile-store.ts`, and is the canonical precedent. Building a parallel module is operational waste; the founder's R17f-driven "must not lock the owner out" concern is better addressed by Decision 4's backup discipline than by domain partitioning.

- **Decision 2 (Option 2A — reuse `MENTOR_ENCRYPTION_KEY`):** single key + single backup runbook + single point of failure but mitigated by the three-copy backup discipline. Domain partitioning (Option 2B) doubles operational load without proportionate safety benefit at single-founder scale. Per-user keys (Option 2C) logged for re-examination at user-base extension (the `encryption.ts` PBKDF2 pattern is the future template for per-user key derivation).

- **Decision 3 (Option 3A — per-column ciphertext + meta):** matches the `mentor-profile-store.ts` precedent's `encrypted_profile` (TEXT) + `encryption_meta` (JSONB: `{ iv, authTag, algorithm, version }`) shape. Selective field decryption is possible; KG7-aware (the JSONB metadata is passed as object, not stringified); schema is self-documenting. Concrete schema specified in ADR §Decision 3 with the noted choice of TEXT (base64 ciphertext) over BYTEA for pattern consistency (implementation session may revisit if profiling shows base64 overhead is meaningful — R17b-compliant either way).

- **Decision 4 (Option 4A — founder three-copy offline backup):** the load-bearing R17f decision. Three independent copies (password manager + printed paper + Vercel env var); monthly verification cadence; mutual-exclusion failure modes. Plain-language for the non-coder founder included in the ADR (what to do at key generation; what to do at password-manager loss; what to do at Vercel access loss). KMS migration (Option 4B) deferred to scale (≥100 users or first compliance trigger).

- **Decision 5 (env-flag-based rollback path):** three paths in order of preference — Path A (flip `MENTOR_RAG_V1=false` within minutes; new route stops engaging new tables; existing pipeline unaffected); Path B (DROP TABLE — reversible because no historical data exists pre-build per PR1); Path C (key restoration from the three-copy backup). The implementation session's Critical Change Protocol uses these paths verbatim.

**Critical Change Protocol responses pre-drafted (for the eventual implementation session, per R17f):**

The ADR drafts the implementation session's Critical Change Protocol responses verbatim:
- **What is changing:** new tables + new helpers; no existing data touched; no existing route changed; same algorithm + same key the application already uses for mentor profiles.
- **What could break:** wrong env var (mitigation: pre-deploy `isServerEncryptionConfigured()` check); schema mismatch (mitigation: staging mirror); decrypt failure key mismatch (mitigation: dry-run encrypt-then-decrypt test); JSONB double-stringification (KG7 mitigation: helpers pass meta object directly to Supabase + post-deploy `jsonb_typeof` check); AC7 surface accidentally engaged (mitigation: zero edits to middleware/auth/cookie config; module-level only).
- **What happens to existing sessions:** Nothing. Additive change. No cookie/session/redirect behaviour touched. AC7 standing constraint NOT engaged.
- **Rollback plan:** three paths per Decision 5 above.
- **Verification step (post-deploy, founder-performable):** `curl /api/health` for encryption-configured indicator; route-level canonical test; SQL `jsonb_typeof` + `length(encrypted_payload)` checks.
- **Explicit approval required:** founder approval specific to the named risks (not generic "yes proceed").
