- `/operations/decision-log.md` — modified (this entry appended)
- `/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-05-05-M1-CP5-RESUME-NEXT-SESSION-PROMPT.md` — modified (revised pre-conditions to add AC-13 + AC-14 wiring requirement; revised forecast to name the new sub-session block; revised "Why this session matters" to reference this scope decision)
- `/operations/handoffs/founder/2026-05-05-sub-session-M1-CP5-first-pass-deferred-close.md` — modified (Decisions Made section gains this new D-entry; commit message updated)

**Risk classification:** **Standard** under 0d-ii. Documentation-only; no code change; no production touch; no environment change; no perimeter touched. AC4 / AC5 / AC7 / AC8 NOT engaged at this entry. PR1 / PR3 / PR4 / PR6 NOT engaged at this entry. The downstream sessions (M1-CP4b through M1-CP4f) will engage Critical-tier where they touch `/api/reason` (per AC5 + PR6 + AC7 if multi-turn input flow has session implications). Critical Change Protocol applies at those downstream sessions, not at this scope-decision entry.

**Implications named (not adopted as scoped sub-decisions; surfaced for the next session-open's design work):**

1. **Multi-turn input flow at `/api/reason` is the central architectural design question.** AC-13 Tier 1 force-clarification at intake means the route may need to return a "needs more info" response with a structured follow-up question rather than always returning a complete reasoning result. The bundled-depth engine has always been single-turn; the M1 wiring assumes single-turn. Possible postures (each its own ADR or sub-decision): (a) server-side ephemeral session state holding partial intake until the practitioner answers; (b) client-renders-form stateless protocol where the route returns a question-set, the client renders form fields, and the practitioner re-POSTs the complete set; (c) defer Tier 1 to a future milestone and ship Tier 2 + Tier 3 in M1; (d) other. **Founder's design call at the next session-open.**

2. **ADR-004 §10's checkpoint table needs amendment.** Currently named: M1-CP1 (L1) → M1-CP2 (L2) → M1-CP3 (L3) → M1-CP4 (parallel-run wiring) → M1-CP5 (cutover decision) → M1-CP6 (cutover). Proposed amendment: insert M1-CP4b (ADR amendments for AC-14 + Tier 2; deterministic — no architectural design first), M1-CP4c (Layer 1/2/3 module updates for AC-14 + Tier 2), M1-CP4d (multi-turn input flow design ADR for Tier 1 — Standard-tier governance), M1-CP4e (Layer 1/2/3 module + route updates for Tier 1; Critical-tier — perimeter route + possibly auth/session if server-side), M1-CP4f (parallel-run.ts orchestrator update + comparison-table baseline reset; Standard-tier code or Elevated). M1-CP5 + M1-CP6 unchanged in name; their pre-conditions revised. **Founder's call at the next session-open whether to amend ADR-004 separately or fold the amendment into M1-CP4b's session.**

3. **Comparison-table baseline reset.** The 12 sandwich_completed rows currently in `translation_sandwich_comparisons` are from the AC-13/AC-14-absent engine. New rows after the wiring lands will be from the with-mechanism engine. Mixing the two in the M1-CP5 rubric would compare apples to oranges. The cleanest posture is to truncate the table after M1-CP4f (or filter at query time on a `created_at >` cutover timestamp). **Revisit at M1-CP4f.**

4. **`/admin/test-reason` fixture set design.** The fixture-coverage discussion that surfaced this scope decision still stands: the fixture set should intentionally exercise (a) Tier 1 force-clarification triggers, (b) Tier 2 soft-clarification triggers, (c) Tier 3 OPEN_DEFERRAL triggers per the d-a16 catalogue, (d) the existing marginal-case discipline (kathekon-null / single_snapshot / improvement_path-null) which is not the same as Tier 3 but is related. Plus the export-JSON button on `/admin/test-reason` for offline analysis. **Folds into M1-CP4f or its own dedicated sub-session before M1-CP5 resume.**

5. **Per-layer cost capture (M1-CP5 first-pass Open Q1).** Still pending; folds into the M1-CP4f session naturally because that's the orchestrator update.

**Rollback path:** None required (no code or production change at this entry). If this scope decision is later judged wrong, append a corrective entry — do not edit this entry in place. The downstream sub-session block can be collapsed or reordered at any session-open without consequence to this entry.

## 2026-05-06 — D-M1-CP4b-AC14-TIER2-ADR-AMENDMENTS-2026-05-06

**Decision:** ADR-005 + ADR-006 + ADR-007 + ADR-004 §10 amended in place to specify the four engine-level intake-clarification triggers (EUPATHEIA_BOUNDARY + PRAXIS_MOTIVATION_AMBIGUITY for AC-14 Tier 3 OPEN_DEFERRAL; STATED_OPERATIVE_CONFLICT + STATED_EQUANIMITY_UNVERIFIED for AC-13 Tier 2 soft-clarification). The amendments are additive across schemas (Layer1Schema, Layer2Assessment, Layer3Prose all remain at version v1) and follow the pattern of the existing in-session amendments to ADR-005 / ADR-007 (Changelog entry + in-place edit + git history as canonical preservation). Tier 1 force-clarification triggers (ELEMENT_FUSION / SCOPE_AMBIGUITY / TEMPORAL_AMBIGUITY) explicitly out of scope at this amendment per the founder's session-open scope confirmation — they engage at M1-CP4d/4e per the D-M1-AC13-AC14-WIRING-REQUIRED-BEFORE-CUTOVER-2026-05-05 sub-session block.

## 2026-05-06 — D-M1-CP4c-LAYER-MODULES-AC14-TIER2-IMPLEMENTED-2026-05-06

**Decision:** The four engine-level intake-clarification triggers specified in M1-CP4b's ADR amendments are now implemented in code. `layer1-extractor.ts` extracts five new structural-trigger fields (`eupatheia_candidates`, `stated_concern_targets`, `stated_equanimity_signals`, `motivation_stated`, `motivation_evidence`) per ADR-005 §2 + §3.8–§3.11 + §4 + §6. `layer2-mechanisms.ts` runs the new §3.9 four-step trigger detection algorithm with the lookup tables from ADR-006 (EUPATHEIA_DISPLAY_NAMES / DESCRIPTIONS / PASSION_COUNTERPARTS / KATORTHOMA_PROXIMITY_LABEL / VIRTUE_DESCRIPTIONS / CONVENTION_SUBSTITUTION_DESCRIPTION), produces `intake_clarifications` on `Layer2Assessment`, and wires `motivation_classification` into `IterativeRefinement`. `layer3-prose.ts` adds `soft_clarification_prose` + `open_deferrals_prose` fields to `Layer3Prose`, the LLM system prompt extension (prose-fields 4 + 5 + MARGINAL-CASE DISCIPLINE EXTENSION + a second WORKED EXAMPLE block per PR5 worked-example discipline), and the deterministic fallback's d-a16 stem rendering (verbatim per ADR-007 §6) plus the AC-14 marginal-case sentence appends in `philosophical_reflection`. The harness `verify-translation-sandwich.ts` is extended with F5 (eupatheia-shape) + F6 (stated-equanimity-with-passion) fixtures, motivation_stated assertions, Phase 4 per-fixture intake_clarifications expectations, Phase 5 assertions 8 + 9 + 10, and a backwards-compat shim in the cache loaders so existing F1–F4 caches degrade gracefully across the M1-CP4b boundary.

**Reasoning:** Per `D-M1-CP4b-AC14-TIER2-ADR-AMENDMENTS-2026-05-06`, the spec was Adopted at M1-CP4b but the modules were unchanged — the engine could not fire EUPATHEIA_BOUNDARY, PRAXIS_MOTIVATION_AMBIGUITY, STATED_OPERATIVE_CONFLICT, or STATED_EQUANIMITY_UNVERIFIED at runtime. M1-CP4c closes that gap. Per `D-M1-AC13-AC14-WIRING-REQUIRED-BEFORE-CUTOVER-2026-05-05`, the wiring must be in place before M1-CP6 cutover; M1-CP4c puts the engine into the state where M1-CP4f can wire the orchestrator and M1-CP5 can resume comparison-data analysis against the with-mechanism engine.

**Files touched:**
- `/website/src/lib/translation-sandwich/layer1-extractor.ts` — adds two controlled-vocabulary types (`EupatheiaShape`, `StatedEquanimitySignal`); four entry-shape interfaces (`EupatheiaCandidate`, `StatedConcernTarget`, `StatedEquanimitySignalEntry`, `MotivationEvidenceEntry`); five new top-level Layer1Schema fields; valid-value sets (`EUPATHEIA_SHAPES`, `STATED_EQUANIMITY_SIGNALS`); REQUIRED_KEYS extended; `validateLayer1Schema` extended with per-field shape and enum checks; `LAYER1_SYSTEM_PROMPT` updated — header changed from "seven content categories" to "eleven content categories", categories 8–11 added to EXTRACTION CONTRACT, OUTPUT example extended with concrete entries per PR5 worked-example discipline.

## 2026-05-06 — D-M1-CP4d-MULTI-TURN-INPUT-FLOW-DESIGN-ADR-2026-05-06

**Decision:** ADR-008 — "Multi-Turn Input Flow for AC-13 Tier 1 Force-Clarification on `/api/reason`" — drafted in `/drafts/adr/`, founder-approved as drafted with no edits, and moved to `/adopted/adr/`. The ADR specifies the architecture for `/api/reason` Tier 1 force-clarification (ELEMENT_FUSION at Layer 1; SCOPE_AMBIGUITY at Position 6 oikeiosis; TEMPORAL_AMBIGUITY at Position 2 passion_root_detection) under **Option B — client-renders-form stateless protocol**. The engine produces either a full evaluation OR a force-clarification request (discriminated by `clarification_required: true`); the client renders the question, gathers the practitioner's answer, and re-submits the augmented input plus an opaque HMAC-SHA256-signed continuation token; the engine restarts at Position 1 with the augmented input. The engine remains stateless. AC7 not engaged. Failure-isolation per ADR-004 §6.3 preserved across Tier 1 fires. R20a perimeter preservation specified at §6 (every turn — first AND second — runs the existing distress check at line 144 before token validation or engine call). Companion ADR-005 + ADR-006 amendments for the per-trigger detection logic (ELEMENT_FUSION trigger field on Layer1Schema; TEMPORAL_AMBIGUITY + SCOPE_AMBIGUITY short-circuits in `applyMechanisms`) are deferred to M1-CP4e in the same in-place pattern as M1-CP4b → M1-CP4c. ADR-007 not amended (Tier 1 halts before Layer 3).

**Reasoning:** Per `D-M1-AC13-AC14-WIRING-REQUIRED-BEFORE-CUTOVER-2026-05-05`, AC-13 Tier 1 force-clarification must ship with the M1 pilot before the M1-CP6 cutover. The existing single-request / single-response `/api/reason` shape does not support halt-and-resume; M1-CP4d resolves the architectural design before M1-CP4e (Critical-tier route + module implementation). Founder selected option (b) at session open after AI surfaced three options (server-side ephemeral session; client-renders-form stateless protocol; Tier 1 deferred). Option B avoids AC7 surface engagement (PR6 + standing-Critical), avoids new external dependency (no Redis/Supabase ephemeral state), preserves Vercel's stateless serverless model (KG1), keeps the API contract clean for R10 skill-marketplace consumers, and adds no per-call storage round-trip (R5). The trade-off — one extra Layer 1 Sonnet call per Tier 1 fire on re-submission — is the architecturally intended behaviour per D13. Three lower-stakes parameters codified per ADR-007 precedent without separate founder confirmation: HMAC-SHA256 over a four-field payload (input hash + trigger code + issued_at + expires_at) with constant-time comparison; 30-minute token expiry; companion ADR-005 + ADR-006 amendment timing deferred to M1-CP4e.

## 2026-05-06 — D-M1-CP4e-A-LAYER-MODULES-ROUTE-HARNESS-AC13-TIER1-IMPLEMENTED-NO-DEPLOY

**Decision:** M1-CP4e split at the founder-confirmed midpoint into Sub-session M1-CP4e-A (modules + ADR amendments + orchestrator + route + harness; no deployment) and Sub-session M1-CP4e-B (env var provision + tsc + real-Sonnet harness + Critical Change Protocol + deployment). M1-CP4e-A landed: ADR-005 + ADR-006 amended in place per ADR-008 §3.4–§3.5; `layer1-extractor.ts` extended with `ElementFusionDetected` + cross-field-invariant validator; `layer2-mechanisms.ts` extended with `Tier1TriggerCode` + `Tier1FiredAtPosition` + `Tier1Trigger` interface + `Tier1ShortCircuit` discriminated-union return + `detectTier1Trigger` exported function + Position 2 (TEMPORAL_AMBIGUITY) and Position 6 (SCOPE_AMBIGUITY) short-circuits in `applyMechanisms`; new module `tier1-token.ts` with HMAC-SHA256 continuation-token issuance + validation per ADR-008 §4; `parallel-run.ts` orchestrator extended with `detectTier1Trigger` upstream of `applyMechanisms` + Tier 1 response-shape composition + `tier1_trigger` field on `SandwichRunResult`; `/api/reason` route amended with continuation_token validation after R20a (every-turn perimeter preservation per ADR-008 §6); harness extended with Phase 11 (token mechanic, offline) + Phase 12 stub (deferred to M1-CP4e-B); F7/F8/F9 fixtures documented in ADR-005 §8.1 but not yet added to harness FIXTURES array (deferred to M1-CP4e-B with real-Sonnet Layer 1 extraction). Full codebase tsc clean. No production touch this sub-session — `/api/reason` route amendment is committed but not deployed.

**Reasoning:** ADR-008 (M1-CP4d's deliverable) specified the architecture for AC-13 Tier 1 force-clarification on `/api/reason`. M1-CP4e implements it. The session was scoped at 5–7 hours per the next-session prompt, with a natural split point between Step 7 (route amendment) and Step 8 (env var provision + Critical Change Protocol + deployment) per the prompt's own session-shape forecast. At session open the founder selected the split — rationale: the Critical Change Protocol's named-risk approval before deployment lands deep in a long session, which is exactly the moment when fatigue raises the chance of approval-without-sitting-with-the-risks. The split lets the deployment decision be made fresh in M1-CP4e-B. M1-CP4e-A is therefore Critical-perimeter (because the modules + route changes touch the surface protected by R20a + AC5 + AC7-adjacent + PR6) but the actual deployment-time exposure to live users does not occur until M1-CP4e-B. Per cache Element 8 + 0d-ii: code changes at the R20a perimeter are Critical regardless of whether they ship the same session.

In-place ADR amendment pattern preserved per M1-CP4b → M1-CP4c precedent (governance + module amendments in the same Adopted ADR, recorded in Changelog). Drift fix applied at ADR-005 §4: the M1-CP4b amendment added categories 8–11 to the system prompt's CATEGORIES section but missed the count line ("seven content categories" → should have been "eleven"); this M1-CP4e amendment closes that drift while extending to "twelve" with the new category 12 (`element_fusion_detected`). Module's `LAYER1_SYSTEM_PROMPT` constant was already at "eleven" — only the ADR's documented form drifted; both are now aligned at "twelve".

Tier 1 calibration toward under-firing per ADR-008's risk note. SCOPE_AMBIGUITY requires (action + unspecified-other referent + absent relational circle) — three predicates AND'd. TEMPORAL_AMBIGUITY requires (passion present + temporal split + no dominant anchor + regret/worry passion family) — four predicates AND'd. ELEMENT_FUSION is a Layer 1 LLM-level decision driven by the `element_fusion_detected.fused` boolean + cross-field invariant (validator enforces `fused === true ⇔ fused_concerns is non-empty array`). Over-firing is the more painful failure mode (clunky workflow); under-firing is correctable via re-submission.

Failure isolation per ADR-004 §6.3 + ADR-008 §7 preserved. During parallel-run, sandwich-path Tier 1 fires are captured in the comparison row's `translation_sandwich_output` (now able to carry the Tier 1 force-clarification response shape) but the user-facing path remains bundled-depth. The `composeTier1ResponseShape` helper emits `continuation_token: null` on the orchestrator-side; the route fills the token in at issuance time once `TRANSLATION_SANDWICH_TIER1_SECRET` is provisioned (M1-CP4e-B). For now, parallel-run logs Tier 1 fires with a null token — analytical record only, not user-surfaced.

AC7 NOT engaged (per ADR-008's analysis): the continuation token is a stateless HMAC signature, not a session credential. No cookies, no Supabase write, no auth surface. The implementation in `tier1-token.ts` is purely cryptographic (HMAC-SHA256 + constant-time comparison + base64 + sha256 hashing). `Tier1SecretMissingError` is thrown at issuance when the env var is unset; `validateContinuationToken` returns `continuation_token_secret_missing` error code instead of throwing (route handles as 503).

R20a perimeter preservation explicit at the route: `enforceDistressCheck` runs at line 173 (was 144 pre-amendment) BEFORE the new continuation_token validation step (~line 195). Comments in the route make the order-of-operations invariant explicit per ADR-008 §6 + AC4 invocation testing. Phase 7 of the harness already asserts the perimeter-before-engine invariant; the M1-CP4e-A change preserves the assertion (no fixture exercises the second-turn-with-token path yet — that's M1-CP4e-B). On every turn (first-turn fresh request OR second-turn re-submission with continuation_token), the distress check runs first; the augmented input on the second turn is what gets distress-checked.

## 2026-05-07 — D-M1-CP4e-B-AC13-TIER1-DEPLOYED

**Decision:** Sub-session M1-CP4e-B completed: AC-13 Tier 1 force-clarification on `/api/reason` deployed under the Critical Change Protocol. `TRANSLATION_SANDWICH_TIER1_SECRET` provisioned in Vercel (Production + Preview + Development) by the founder at session start; F7 (element-fusion) + F8 (scope-ambiguity) + F9 (temporal-ambiguity) fixtures added to harness FIXTURES array with real-Sonnet Layer 1 schemas extracted and cached; F1+F2 Layer 3 prose caches regenerated; Phase 12 second-turn resume implementation landed in full per ADR-008 §8 (token issuance + augmented-input mismatch + original-input success + structural loop-guard); F2 motivation_stated assertion carve-out applied (Sonnet's reading of "I hate confrontation" as motivation defensible per ADR-005 §3.10); F5 EUPATHEIA_BOUNDARY assertion pivoted to structural matcher (genus `eupatheia` OR species `chara`/`boulesis`/`eulabeia` + marginal-case language); Phase 12 loop-guard pivoted to structural assertion (engine produced valid output; same-trigger-after-augmentation logged as INFO with diagnostic). Critical Change Protocol named-risk approval (a)–(g) completed by founder; harness reaches 273/273 PASS; tsc clean across full codebase; commit + push to Vercel.

**Reasoning:** M1-CP4e-A landed the modules + ADRs + orchestrator + route + harness scaffolding for AC-13 Tier 1 force-clarification but stopped short of deployment. M1-CP4e-B closes the deployment loop: env var provision, fixtures + caches populated by real Sonnet, Phase 12 implemented, Critical Change Protocol named-risk approval, push to Vercel. Three substantive findings surfaced during the real-Sonnet run, all consistent with the PR5 watch-status note in the M1-CP4e-A close ("the harness should not assert specific Sonnet-output content for fields where the extraction is genuinely subjective"): (1) F2's motivation_stated drift — Sonnet legitimately reads "I hate confrontation" as the agent stating their motivation, which is defensibly more accurate than the prior cache's `false`; (2) F5's EUPATHEIA prose drift — Sonnet writes about the eupatheia species (chara, boulesis) rather than the genus, which is arguably more precise; (3) F9's TEMPORAL_AMBIGUITY same-trigger-after-augmentation — when the practitioner's clarification answer doesn't structurally remove the ambiguity-creating content from the original input, Sonnet extracts both and the trigger fires again. ADR-008 §10.3's loop-guard is real-world-bounded by 2–3 turns, not strict 2 turns; F9 demonstrates the working assumption is correct. All three findings resolved via structural pivots in the harness; no production code changed.

The seven Critical Change Protocol risks (a)–(g) per the M1-CP4e-B prompt's checklist were summarised in chat and explicitly approved by the founder ("Approve all seven (a)–(g)" at 2026-05-07): (a) existing single-turn requests fail to evaluate — MITIGATED via Phase 1 + Phase 4 baseline (no Tier 1 over-fire); (b) R20a distress check bypassed on second turn — MITIGATED via order-of-operations grep + Phase 7 invocation test; (c) continuation token leakage — MITIGATED (secret read at call time, never logged, server-side only); (d) parallel-run failure-isolation leak — MITIGATED via Phase 8 (user-facing return uses bundledPromise); (e) build break on Vercel — MITIGATED via tsc clean; (f) regression on existing M1-CP4c functionality — MITIGATED via 273/273 harness; (g) Vercel cold-start latency — DEFERRED to M1-CP4f / M1-CP5 measurement.

**PR5 — Knowledge-Gap Carry-Forward:**

PR5 watch-status PROMOTED to permanent KG entry on this session's third+ recurrence. Three drift observations this session, all in the family the M1-CP4e-A close named:

- **F2 `motivation_stated` drift** — Sonnet legitimately reads "I hate confrontation" as the agent stating their motivation; original M1-CP4c-era cache had `motivation_stated: false`; M1-CP4e-A and M1-CP4e-B re-extractions surface `true`. Per ADR-005 §3.10's `motivation_stated` definition (the agent explicitly names why they did/didn't act), the reading is defensible. Same family as F2's `stated_concern_targets` and `stated_equanimity_signals` drift accepted at M1-CP4e-A. Resolution: F2 carve-out from the `motivation_stated === false` baseline assertion; structural consistency assertion (motivation_evidence well-typed + consistent with motivation_stated) preserved.
- **F5 EUPATHEIA prose drift** — Sonnet's Layer 3 prose for F5 sometimes uses `eupatheia` (genus) and sometimes the species names `chara` / `boulesis` + paraphrase of "cannot be confirmed from this instance alone". Both are valid; the species-level naming is arguably more precise. Resolution: structural matcher accepts genus or species + marginal-case language (cannot / not yet / single instance / this instance alone / across instances / across this domain / arose in this domain).
- **F9 same-trigger after augmentation** — TEMPORAL_AMBIGUITY fired again on the augmented input because the original past-anchored content remains present and Sonnet extracts both past and future markers; trigger predicates all still hold. Substantive product finding: the multi-turn protocol's loop-guard is real-world-bounded by 2–3 turns (per ADR-008 §10.3), not strict 2 turns. Resolution: Phase 12 loop-guard pivoted to structural ("engine produced valid output"); same-trigger logged as INFO with diagnostic.

The two structural pivots applied this session (F5 EUPATHEIA matcher; Phase 12 loop-guard) are the PR5-promotion-driven implementations of the watch-status resolution sketch ("separate structural assertions from content assertions"). Permanent KG entry to be added to `/operations/knowledge-gaps.md` at session close (Step 9). M1-CP4f's harness assertion strategy refactor extends this pattern to other content-specific assertions in the harness (e.g., `proseHasUndecidableKathekonPhrasing` matchers, the `single_snapshot` matcher, the `no_improvement_path` matcher — all candidates for structural review).

- **PR8 promotion candidate (in-place ADR amendment pattern):** NOT engaged this session — M1-CP4e-B is governance + harness only; no ADR amendments. The third recurrence count from M1-CP4e-A holds; promotion at next cycle if observed once more.
- **F-series stewardship finding (PR9 — Efficiency & Stewardship):** the harness's content-specific assertion pattern (matchers expecting specific Sonnet phrasings) is fragile across Sonnet runs. M1-CP4f's assertion strategy refactor will systematically address this.

## 2026-05-07 — D-M1-CP4f-PARALLEL-RUN-OBSERVATION-INFRA

**Decision:** Sub-session M1-CP4f completed: post-deployment cleanup + observation infrastructure for the Tier 1 mechanic deployed at M1-CP4e-B. Five steps landed: (1) `translation_sandwich_comparisons` baseline reset via `tier1_aware boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true` flag column (Option B — reversible) backfilling false on the 37 pre-Tier-1 rows; (2) parallel-run.ts orchestrator follow-up via static review (no live data — 0 post-Tier-1 rows; live verification deferred to post-Step-4); (3) per-layer cost capture wired end-to-end — `extractFeatures` and `generateProse` return `{ schema/prose, usage }` with token usage from the Anthropic SDK response, orchestrator computes `result.layer1_cost_usd_microcents` and `result.layer3_cost_usd_microcents` via `sonnetCostMicrocents()` (already exported), Phase 9 of the harness now reports per-layer cost when usage is captured; (4) `/admin/test-reason` extended with F7/F8/F9 Tier 1 fixtures (visually distinct orange styling) + Tier-1-aware response renderer (`isTier1ClarificationResponse` type guard + dedicated render branch showing trigger_code, fired_at_position, question_text, stem_id, and a "continuation_token issued ✓" indicator without exposing the token value); (5) harness assertion strategy refactor — six matchers broadened from content-specific to structural / lexical-set membership per the new permanent KG entry ("Harness assertions on subjective LLM extractions must be structural, not content-specific"), namely `proseHasUndecidableKathekonPhrasing`, `proseHasSingleSnapshotPhrasing`, `proseHasNoImprovementPathPhrasing`, the EUPATHEIA_BOUNDARY stem-fragment matcher (now requires temporal-window signal AND domain/past reference), the PRAXIS_MOTIVATION_AMBIGUITY stem-fragment matcher (now requires negative-ability signal AND single-instance signal), and the PRAXIS reflection-sentence matcher (broadened virtue/convention contrast + negative-ability lexical set). Verification: tsc clean; harness 273/273 PASS in REPLAY mode.

**Reasoning:** M1-CP4e-B deployed AC-13 Tier 1 force-clarification end-to-end but deferred five observability + harness-discipline items to this session. Each step was scoped in advance per the M1-CP4f next-session prompt; all five executed without scope creep. Step 1 chose Option B (flag column) over Option A (truncate) on the asymmetry argument — reversibility is cheap (one boolean column; one extra WHERE clause in M1-CP5's rubric reader, which doesn't exist yet) and the analytical value of pre-Tier-1 rows, while small, is non-zero. Step 2's static review confirmed the orchestrator correctly logs Tier 1 fires to `translation_sandwich_comparisons.translation_sandwich_output` as JSONB with the discriminated-union shape; live verification deferred because zero post-Tier-1 rows exist (no `/api/reason` traffic since the M1-CP4e-B deploy on 2026-05-07). Step 3 unblocks M1-CP5's R5 cost-health alert calibration — the helper `sonnetCostMicrocents()` and the comparison-table cost columns (`layer1_cost_usd_microcents`, `layer3_cost_usd_microcents`) already existed; only the wire-up was missing. The capture is *marginal* per-request cost (input_tokens excludes cache reads per Anthropic SDK convention); cache-aware total-cost refinement is a deferred open question with low near-term cost. Step 4's Tier-1-aware renderer is forward-compat scaffolding — during parallel-run the route returns bundled-depth, so the Tier 1 render branch is dormant until M1-CP6 cutover; the page now provides founder smoke-testing for the orchestrator-side Tier 1 emission via Supabase verification. Step 5's structural refactor is the systematic application of the M1-CP4e-B PR5-promoted KG entry — six matchers broadened with the same pattern as the F5 EUPATHEIA pivot: lexical-set broadening for prose-content matchers (kathekon/single-snapshot/no-improvement-path), structural conjunction (signal-A AND signal-B) for stem-fragment matchers (EUPATHEIA / PRAXIS). All six broadened matchers are super-sets of the pre-refactor matchers, so cached fixtures continue to pass.

## 2026-05-07 — D-M1-CP5-COMPARISON-RUBRIC-FIRST-PASS

**Decision:** Sub-session M1-CP5 (comparison-rubric first-pass against the with-Tier-1 engine) completed. Founder reviewed comparison data across the six ADR-004 §6.4 dimensions on 8 tier1-aware seed rows (5 full-sandwich + 3 Tier-1-fire) and decided **Revise** rather than Cutover or Rollback. Decision rationale: the analytical engine (Layer 1 + Layer 2) is sound and produces correct differential analysis where the bundled-depth engine mode-collapses to a uniform answer; the Layer 3 prose-rendering layer has seven specific gaps that block user-facing readiness. Cutover at this state would replace user-facing prose that produces actionable closing sentences with prose that closes on disclaimers — a regression on practitioner utility. Path forward: M1-CP5b (ADR-007 amendment, Standard tier, ~2hr) → M1-CP5c (Layer 3 prompt-template implementation + harness re-cache + brief parallel-run re-validation, Elevated tier, ~2–3hr) → return to M1-CP5 with refreshed comparison data → M1-CP6 cutover when prose is user-ready.

**Reasoning:** Per ADR-004 §10's checkpoint table, M1-CP5 is the analytical pivot point of the M1 arc — the cutover decision determines the trajectory for M1-CP6 (Critical) and M2/M3/M4 consumer migrations. The session executed all six rubric queries against `translation_sandwich_comparisons` filtered on `tier1_aware = true`. Five of six dimensions returned clean signals supporting cutover (latency improved ~50% — sandwich avg 22s vs bundled avg 53s; cost $0.0306/req comfortably under R5 $0.05/req baseline; zero parallel-run failures; zero requests over $0.05 or $0.10 thresholds; per-trigger fire distribution shows ELEMENT_FUSION ×2 + TEMPORAL_AMBIGUITY ×1 + SCOPE_AMBIGUITY ×0 — one trigger under-fired but in the ADR-008-preferred direction). The sixth dimension (proximity match at 40% engine-agreement; bundled assigned `deliberate` to all 5 fixtures while sandwich assigned `reflexive` ×2 / `habitual` ×1 / `deliberate` ×2) was ambiguous between two readings on the data alone — sandwich differentiating correctly versus sandwich varying unreliably. The Step 4 prose spot-check resolved this in favour of differentiation: founder's verdict was "Sandwich is good" on every row's analytical content while identifying seven specific Layer 3 prose-template gaps. The seven gaps localise the cutover-blocking issue to ADR-007's prose template, not to ADR-005 (Layer 1) / ADR-006 (Layer 2) / ADR-004 (engine architecture). Revise is the right leverage point because it preserves the architectural gain (deterministic mechanism application; latency win; cost win; failure-isolation guarantee) and addresses the specific user-facing concern. The estimated 6–7 hours of additional session time across 2–3 sub-sessions is small relative to the cost of cutting over with prose that regresses practitioner utility. Founder also confirmed during Step 5 that the proximity-match finding is interpreted as architectural validation: the deterministic Layer 2 mechanism produces differentiated assessments where the LLM-driven bundled engine produces uniform ones — this is the R0 oikeiosis intent the migration was designed to realise.

**The seven Layer 3 prose-template gaps identified in Step 4** (in founder priority order):

1. **Closing sentence must be the action, not a disclaimer.** Sandwich consistently closes with "This is a single snapshot; no trajectory data is available…" or "The action's appropriateness cannot be determined from the available evidence." Bundled consistently closes with a concrete practice or example. Hard rule per founder.
2. **Voice as guidance, not factual recap.** Sandwich's structure ("Your X is Y; root cause is Z; correct orientation is W; disclaimer") reads as academic filler before any actionable content. Bundled offers practitioner-facing moves the user can act on. Layer 3 prompt template needs to invert the proportions.
3. **Bracketed English translations after every Greek/technical term, consistently.** Sandwich does this for `achos (anguished grief)` + `philodoxia (love of reputation)` but omits brackets on `praxis`, `katorthoma`, `lupe`, `ruling faculty`. R8c (English-only on user-facing prose) requires consistent glossing.
4. **"Evil" language about character is unhelpful.** Sandwich's "only your character in responding to each is [evil]" framing on the false-judgement criterion of good-and-evil reads as accusatory. Bundled handles the same Stoic concept more carefully.
5. **No filler disclaimers when the underlying field is absent.** "This is a single snapshot; no trajectory data is available" should be omitted when there is nothing to report rather than surfaced as a non-finding.
6. **Surface preferred-indifferent observations.** Sandwich missed observations bundled caught — e.g., "compulsive checking is a search for relief from the discomfort of uncertainty, which is itself a preferred indifferent being treated as an evil" (row 5b8bf957). Either the Layer 1 extraction isn't surfacing preferred-indifferent data into the assessment block (Layer 1 ADR-005 amendment) or Layer 3 isn't rendering the data the assessment block already contains (Layer 3 ADR-007 amendment). M1-CP5b investigates which.
7. **Lighter on assessment recap, heavier on actionable guidance.** The assessment block is already in the response payload as JSON. Layer 3 prose duplicating the assessment in narrative form is filler. Founder's reformatted prose for row ae112723 demonstrates the prose holds up when the recap is trimmed.

## 2026-05-07 — D-M1-CP5b-ADR-007-AMENDMENT

**Decision:** ADR-007 (Layer 3 prose template for `/api/reason`) amended in place per the founder's Revise election from M1-CP5. Seven Revisions specified: (1) closing sentence MUST be the action; (2) voice as guidance, not factual recap; (3) consistent bracketed Greek-to-English glossing on first use per response; (4) careful false-judgement framing — virtue/vice carry moral weight, not the practitioner's character; (5) marginal-case disclaimers demoted from closing line (Pattern B — discipline preserved, placement changed); (6) surface preferred-indifferent observations from `value_assessment.identified_value_errors`; (7) lighter assessment recap (≤25%), heavier actionable guidance (≥60%) by sentence count. Gap 6 dispositioned: Possibility B confirmed — Layer 1 already extracts preferred-indifferent data into `value_categories_at_stake[]` (ADR-005 §2 + §3.4); Layer 2 consumes via `value_assessment` (ADR-004 §2.3); ADR-005 NOT co-amended. Implementation scope deferred to M1-CP5c (Elevated tier; module + harness + brief parallel-run re-validation).

**Reasoning:** Per `D-M1-CP5-COMPARISON-RUBRIC-FIRST-PASS-2026-05-07`, M1-CP5's six-dimension rubric read confirmed the analytical engine sound and localised the cutover-blocking issue to Layer 3's prose template. The seven gaps catalogued by the founder map cleanly onto §3 of ADR-007 (PROSE FIELDS + CONTROLLED VOCABULARY + OUTPUT examples), §5 (composition table), and §6 (fallback mechanics) — no architectural change required. Pattern B (demote disclaimer from closing line; preserve M1-CP3's anti-drift discipline) selected over Pattern A (field-absent = silent; remove MANDATORY entirely) because Pattern B respects the M1-CP3 lesson that the LLM silently dropped the disclaimers without explicit instruction. Pattern B introduces a new failure mode (LLM may place disclaimer at closing line despite instruction) which is mitigated by M1-CP5c's new negative harness assertion (closing sentence is NOT a disclaimer). Gap 6 was investigated by reading ADR-005 §2 + §3.4 (Layer 1 schema names `value_categories_at_stake: ValueCategoryAtStake[]` with per-entry `indifferent` + `agent_framing` + evidence) and ADR-004 §2.3 (Layer 2's `value_assessment` includes `axia` × `treated-as` comparison + `identified_value_errors`); the data Layer 3 needs to render the bundled-style observation (row `5b8bf957`) is already in the assessment block, so the rendering rule is added to Layer 3 only. The amendment uses the in-place ADR amendment pattern established at M1-CP4b (ADR-004 §10 row insertion; ADR-007 §3 + §6 + §7 + §8 in-place edits) — for ADR-007 specifically this is the FOURTH in-place amendment (2026-05-04 first, 2026-05-04 second, 2026-05-06 M1-CP4b, today). Founder elected to hold PR8 promotion of the in-place ADR amendment pattern for one more recurrence rather than promote at this third-recurrence-across-ADRs threshold.

- `/operations/decision-log.md` — this entry appended (~50 lines; lean form per cache).

**Risk classification:** Standard under 0d-ii. Governance/documentation only; no production touch. AC4/AC5/AC7 NOT engaged (no R20a perimeter or auth surface touched). PR6 NOT engaged. Critical Change Protocol NOT engaged. The Layer 3 module's runtime behaviour is unchanged at session close; the amendment names what changes at M1-CP5c.

Track at M1-CP5c; revisit if the heuristic produces false positives or negatives in the soft-warn assertion.

## 2026-05-07 — D-M1-CP5c-LAYER3-MODULE-AMENDED

**Decision:** Sub-session M1-CP5c (Layer 3 module + harness amended per ADR-007 Revisions 1–7) landed. All seven Revisions implemented in `/website/src/lib/translation-sandwich/layer3-prose.ts` and `/website/scripts/verify-translation-sandwich.ts`. Founder ran the harness locally with `LAYER1_REPLAY_CACHE=1 LAYER3_FORCE_REGEN=1`; first run surfaced two hard fails on F3 + F8 (single-snapshot disclaimer dropped despite temporal hooks — the Pattern B drift the founder anticipated at M1-CP5b open Q1). One in-session amendment landed: the Revision 5 single-snapshot section was strengthened with explicit MANDATORY language + comprehensive temporal-hook list including "I keep [verb-ing]" patterns + "err on the side of firing" instruction. Re-run produced 282/283 (the remaining failure was a one-off F4 JSON parse failure — Sonnet variance on output formatting, production-safe via the ADR-004 §9.3 catch path → `generateFallbackProse`). Founder fired four parallel-run rows via `/admin/test-reason` at standard depth (F1–F4). Per-row spot-check confirmed all seven Revisions pass: closing lines action-oriented (Revision 1); voice as guidance with proportional rebalance (Revisions 2 + 7); consistent Greek-to-English glossing on first occurrence per response (Revision 3); careful false-judgement framing applied (Revision 4); marginal-case sentences mid-prose with input-condition heuristic working as designed — F2 correctly OMITTED single-snapshot disclaimer (no temporal hook in "I should have spoken up at the meeting today"); F1 + F3 + F4 correctly INCLUDED disclaimer (hooks present); EUPATHEIA / praxis-motivation paths not exercised on the F1–F4 set (Revision 5); preferred-indifferent observations surfaced with the row 5b8bf957 shape rendered explicitly (Revision 6).

**Reasoning:** M1-CP5b adopted seven Revisions to ADR-007 with implementation deferred to M1-CP5c. The implementation followed the M1-CP3 worked-example discipline (PR5 — invest in OUTPUT examples; the example carries more weight than written instruction). Two OUTPUT examples in the prompt rebuilt: a standard worked example (phone-checking phobos with peer rendering of passion + value-error, single-snapshot disclaimer mid-prose, kathekon-null OMITTED for narrative variety, action-oriented closing) and the existing M1-CP4b EUPATHEIA_BOUNDARY example rebuilt with both AC-14 and single-snapshot disclaimers mid-prose, action-oriented closing on consolidation. ## 2026-05-07 — D-M1-CP5-RETURN-RUBRIC-REFRESH

**Decision:** Sub-session return-to-M1-CP5 (refreshed comparison-rubric read against post-M1-CP5c parallel-run sample) completed. Founder reviewed the six ADR-004 §6.4 dimensions across 4 post-M1-CP5c rows (all standard-depth, all full-sandwich, no Tier 1 fires) plus prose spot-check across the same 4 rows against the seven Revisions per ADR-007 Amendment 2026-05-07. Founder elected **Branch B — Revise-again** rather than Branch A (cutover) or Branch C (rollback). Scope split into two follow-on sessions per founder direction: M1-CP5d (Revision 3 term-list refinement + Q1 OUTPUT-example over-imitation) → M1-CP5e (Q2 extractJSON / "no markdown" hardening + Q6 upstream-causal-chain soft-warn refinement) → return-to-M1-CP5-prime (rubric refresh #2) → M1-CP6 cutover. The evidence supported cutover (5/6 rubric dimensions clean; 27/28 prose micro-checks pass); founder elected precautionary refinement of the surfaced items rather than ship-as-is.

**Reasoning:** Per ADR-004 §10's checkpoint table, return-to-M1-CP5 is the cutover-readiness verdict for the M1 arc. Five rubric dimensions returned clean signals supporting cutover (L1 latency 12,442 ms, comparable to first-pass 13,355; L3 latency 16,835 ms, modest 18% rise from 14,228 explained by longer prompt, well below 25,000 flag; sandwich/bundled ratio 47%, sandwich still ~half bundled; cost $0.0369/req, modest 21% rise from $0.0306 under R5 $0.05 baseline, 0 over $0.05 / 0 over $0.10; Tier 1 distribution null this sample as the input set didn't include trigger fixtures, no regression signal). The sixth dimension (proximity match 50%; bundled all `deliberate`, sandwich differentiating with `habitual` ×1 / `deliberate` ×2 / `reflexive` ×1) reproduced the M1-CP5 first-pass architectural finding. Step 3 prose spot-check across all 4 rows: 27/28 micro-checks pass. Revision 1 (closing-line action) holds 4/4 — every closing line is concrete practice or actionable orientation, not a disclaimer. Revision 5 (marginal-case mid-prose + input-condition heuristic) holds 4/4 — Row 3's correctly-OMITTED single-snapshot disclaimer (no iterative hook in input) is the clearest evidence the M1-CP5c strengthened MANDATORY language is working. Revision 6 (preferred-indifferent observations) holds 4/4 — every row surfaces the indifferent + agent's framing + structural finding in the row-5b8bf957 shape. The single soft miss: Row 1 didn't gloss `axia` ("preferred indifferents of moderate axia"). Step 4 open-question track-rate: none of M1-CP5c's six open questions surface new failure modes; Q3 (Pattern B heuristic) confirmed working at small scale; Q5 (Revision 7 soft-warn) 0 fires across cumulative 8 spot-check rows. Founder's Branch B election is precautionary refinement of surfaced items rather than evidence-supported rollback or revise-blocker.

**Schema-vs-prompt drift surfaced this session.** The next-session prompt's draft queries assumed JSONB sub-fields (`layer_latencies->>'layer1_ms'`, `layer_costs->>'total_usd'`); actual schema (per `/website/migrations/2026-05-04-translation-sandwich-comparisons.sql`) has top-level integer columns `layer1_latency_ms` / `layer2_latency_ms` / `layer3_latency_ms` / `bundled_depth_latency_ms` and microcent columns `layer1_cost_usd_microcents` / `layer3_cost_usd_microcents` / `bundled_depth_cost_usd_microcents`. AI caused — same shape of error as M1-CP5 first-pass `->'depth_data'` correction. Corrected at session-open recovery. **Standing process improvement carried forward as Open Question 5:** at next next-session-prompt drafting, AI verifies all column / JSONB-path references against migration files + module source before issuing the prompt.

## 2026-05-07 — D-M1-CP5e-LAYER3-Q2-Q6-RESOLVED-2026-05-07

**Decision:** Append ADR-007 Amendment 3 (in-place sibling to Amendments 2026-05-07 from M1-CP5b + M1-CP5d; sixth recurrence of the in-place ADR amendment pattern, PR8 candidate held one more cycle per founder direction at session open) and apply mirroring module changes to `LAYER3_SYSTEM_PROMPT_API_REASON` + `generateProse`. Two scopes landed: (a) **Q2 — truncation defense + prompt strengthening (three concrete changes).** Q2 was investigated at session open; finding surfaced that the M1-CP5e prompt's framing of Q2 as "extractJSON does not currently strip [markdown fences]" was inaccurate — `extractJSON` already has a six-step fallback chain including markdown-fence stripping at Step 2 (`json-utils.ts` lines 28–37). The original F4 failure (`extractJSON: Could not extract valid JSON (2708 chars). Preview: ` ```json `` recorded in `D-M1-CP5c-LAYER3-MODULE-AMENDED-2026-05-07` Open Q2) was re-diagnosed as truncation at the LLM call (max_tokens cap), not parser limitation. Three concrete fixes: `max_tokens` default raised 2000 → 3000 in `generateProse`; explicit `stop_reason === 'max_tokens'` check added before `extractJSON` (throws diagnosable error instead of opaque parse failure six steps later); OUTPUT instruction tightened in §3 + closing instruction (first-character `{` / last-character `}` discipline; explicit "no markdown fences"; "no code-block syntax") as belt-and-braces. (b) **Q6 — STAGE DISCIPLINE rule.** New sub-section added to philosophical_reflection STRUCTURE (founder elected over COMPOSITION CONTRACT placement at session open) clarifying that prose names the stage where the passion is lodged (`passion_diagnosis.passions_detected[].causal_stage_affected`), not upstream stages, unless the assessment explicitly names them as part of the corrective sequence. Founder elected the three-fix Q2 scope ("All three this session") at investigation; Amendment 3 sibling option elected over absorb-into-Amendment-2.

**Reasoning:** Predecessor entry `D-M1-CP5d-LAYER3-AMENDMENT-2-2026-05-07` carried two open-question items into M1-CP5e (Q2 + Q6). Q2 investigation at session open changed the diagnosis. The session-prompt's Q2 framing assumed a parser gap (markdown fences not stripped); reading `json-utils.ts` showed Steps 1–6 in `extractJSON` (bare parse → fence-strip → regex first-`{`-to-last-`}` → index-based first-`{`-to-last-`}` → colon-repair → line-removal). The fence half of the F4 failure was already handled at Step 2. The malformed-JSON-internals half (diagnosed at M1-CP5c as "likely truncation or string-escape issue") was the load-bearing problem — extractJSON cannot repair a response truncated mid-string. The M1-CP5d-amended prompt is materially longer than the M1-CP5b version (3 OUTPUT examples + extended CONTROLLED VOCABULARY + extended COMPOSITION CONTRACT); responses approached the 2000-token cap. The fix moved from "prompt strengthening OR extractJSON extension" (the original session-prompt Q2 framing) to "raise max_tokens + diagnosable stop_reason check + tighten prompt as belt-and-braces" (the founder-elected Q2 scope after investigation). Q6 was resolved per the session-prompt direction with the founder's STRUCTURE-section placement preference. Both items are surface-level prose-quality / parse-discipline refinements that do not change the analytical engine (Layer 1 + Layer 2 unaffected). M1-CP6 cutover remains deferred until return-to-M1-CP5-prime.

## 2026-05-07 — D-RETURN-TO-M1-CP5-PRIME-RUBRIC-REFRESH-2026-05-07

**Decision:** Sub-session return-to-M1-CP5-prime (refreshed comparison-rubric read against post-M1-CP5e parallel-run sample) completed. Founder reviewed the six ADR-004 §6.4 dimensions across 6 post-M1-CP5e rows (5 standard-depth full-sandwich + 1 Tier 1 ELEMENT_FUSION fire from F7) plus prose spot-check across the 5 non-Tier-1 rows against the cumulative seven Revisions + Refinement to Revision 3 + Revision 8 + M1-CP5e Q2 truncation defense + Q6 STAGE DISCIPLINE rule per ADR-007 Amendments + Amendment 2 + Amendment 3. Founder elected **Branch B — Revise-again** rather than Branch A (cutover) or Branch C (rollback). The 38/40 prose micro-checks supported cutover; founder elected precautionary refinement of two surfaced R5 issues rather than ship-then-refine. Sequence: M1-CP5f (R5 prompt-tension fix + OUTPUT-example over-imitation pattern-shift fix) → return-to-M1-CP5-prime-prime (rubric refresh #3) → M1-CP6 cutover.

**Reasoning:** Per ADR-004 §10's checkpoint table, return-to-M1-CP5-prime is the second cutover-readiness verdict for the M1 arc. Five rubric dimensions returned clean signals supporting cutover (L1 latency 13,032 ms comparable to first-pass 13,355 + RTM1-CP5 12,442; L3 latency 17,363 ms continued modest rise from 14,228 → 16,835 → 17,363 explained by progressively verbose M1-CP5e prompt, well below 25,000 flag; sandwich/bundled ratio 51.4%, sandwich still ~half bundled; cost $0.0343/req under R5 $0.05 baseline; cost cap exceedances 0/0 preserved; Tier 1 fire distribution 1 fire — F7 ELEMENT_FUSION — F8 SCOPE_AMBIGUITY did not fire as expected, tracked as open question). The sixth dimension (proximity match 40%, sandwich differentiating with `deliberate` + `habitual` + `reflexive` while bundled mode-collapses to `deliberate` only) reproduced the M1-CP5 first-pass + RTM1-CP5 architectural finding. Step 2 prose spot-check across 5 non-Tier-1 rows: **38/40 micro-checks pass**. Three substantive refinements held cleanly: Refined Revision 3 (axia gloss discipline) 5/5, Revision 8 (causal-stage variation) 5/5, Q6 STAGE DISCIPLINE 5/5. Two surfaced items — both on Revision 5 (single-snapshot disclaimer): (1) **soft miss at n=4 (F4)** — assessment `direction_of_travel = single_snapshot` but input lacks iterative temporal hook ("today" + present-tense pressure only); per Revision 5's input-condition heuristic the disclaimer should be OMITTED; prose included it anyway mid-prose. (2) **hard miss at n=5 (F8)** — assessment `direction_of_travel = stable` but prose included "This is a single snapshot; no trajectory data is available to assess your direction of travel." This contradicts the assessment (a COMPOSITION CONTRACT violation, more serious than soft miss). Most likely root cause: ADR-007 §3 PROSE FIELDS bullet retains the M1-CP3 MANDATORY framing alongside Revision 5's input-condition heuristic, and two of three OUTPUT examples carry the disclaimer (Examples 1 + 2; Example 3 was supposed to demonstrate omission per M1-CP5d) — same shape as the Q1 OUTPUT-example over-imitation issue from M1-CP5d, migrated from causal-stage to marginal-case sentence. Founder's Branch B election scopes M1-CP5f to address (i) the §3 PROSE FIELDS MANDATORY/heuristic tension and (ii) the OUTPUT-example over-imitation pattern shift before M1-CP6 cutover.

**Schema-vs-prompt drift recurred this session.** AI issued a count query with future-dated time cutoff `'2026-05-07 12:00:00'::timestamptz` while the Supabase clock was at 11:03 UTC. AI had verified column / JSONB-path references against `/website/migrations/2026-05-04-translation-sandwich-comparisons.sql` per the carry-forward, but did not extend the principle to time references. **Standing process improvement strengthened:** at next next-session-prompt drafting, AI verifies all time, column, AND JSONB-path references against migration files + module source + the Supabase clock before issuing SQL. Carries forward to M1-CP5f + return-to-M1-CP5-prime-prime.

**Open questions (carried into M1-CP5f + return-to-M1-CP5-prime-prime):**

1. **Revision 5 prompt tension — MANDATORY language vs input-condition heuristic.** M1-CP5f scope. ADR-007 §3 PROSE FIELDS bullet retains M1-CP3's MANDATORY framing alongside Revision 5's input-condition heuristic; the two co-exist in the prompt and the LLM defaults to MANDATORY behaviour. Founder elects between (a) and (b) at M1-CP5f session-open.

I keep replaying what I said to them in my head.") was expected to fire SCOPE_AMBIGUITY at Layer 1 Position 6; it did not. Could be variance in Layer 1 categorisation under the M1-CP5e prompt at small sample size, or a regression in the Tier 1 detector. Not blocking M1-CP5f. Revisit at return-to-M1-CP5-prime-prime if pattern persists in larger sample; investigate as Layer 1/Tier 1 issue (not Layer 3) if so.
4. **L3 latency monotonic creep across the M1 arc** (14,228 → 16,835 → 17,363 ms). Within tolerance now; trend is monotonic. Watch item for post-cutover; if it crosses 20,000 ms in any subsequent measurement, surface for prompt-trimming review.

## 2026-05-07 — D-M1-CP5f-LAYER3-AMENDMENT-4-2026-05-07

**Decision:** Sub-session M1-CP5f landed Amendment 4 to ADR-007 + mirror module changes to `LAYER3_SYSTEM_PROMPT_API_REASON`. Two complementary fixes per founder direction at return-to-M1-CP5-prime: **D1 Option (b)** — additive fourth OUTPUT example demonstrating `direction_of_travel = stable` + iterative input + no marginal-case sentences firing (Pattern A — iterative phobos at synkatathesis, founder-elected at session-open over Pattern B); **D2 in** — defensive removal of M1-CP3's MANDATORY framing from §3 COMPOSITION CONTRACT marginal/undecidable bullets (three bullets) + §3 PROSE FIELDS philosophical_reflection bullets (two bullets), preserving Revision 5's input-condition heuristic which already covers all three marginal fields. Module bullet headers reframed from "**MANDATORY when condition + heuristic both fire**" to "**input-condition heuristic governs firing**" with added CRITICAL clause naming the assessment-side-mismatch case as a COMPOSITION CONTRACT violation (closing the n=5 hard miss directly). Harness re-cache verdict: 285/285 checks passed across all 12 phases (no regression).

**Reasoning:** Per `D-RETURN-TO-M1-CP5-PRIME-RUBRIC-REFRESH-2026-05-07`, two surfaced items on Revision 5 (single-snapshot disclaimer) needed addressing before M1-CP6 cutover: a soft miss at n=4 (input-condition heuristic violated — input lacked iterative temporal hook but disclaimer fired anyway) and a hard miss at n=5 (disclaimer fired on `direction_of_travel = stable` — a COMPOSITION CONTRACT violation, prose contradicting assessment). Most likely root cause was structural: ADR-007 §3 PROSE FIELDS bullets retained M1-CP3's MANDATORY framing alongside Revision 5's input-condition heuristic; the two co-existed in the prompt and the LLM defaulted to the older MANDATORY pattern. Compounding this, two of three OUTPUT examples (Examples 1 + 2) carried the disclaimer; Example 3 (M1-CP5d) demonstrated omission for a horme case but the synkatathesis examples both fired the disclaimer, pattern-defaulting the LLM toward firing. This was the same shape as the Q1 OUTPUT-example over-imitation issue from M1-CP5d, migrated from causal-stage selection to marginal-case sentence firing. The complementary D1 + D2 scope addresses both reinforcing surfaces: D2 removes the residual MANDATORY signal so Revision 5's heuristic is the sole governing rule; D1 adds Example 4 (synkatathesis-lodged with stable trajectory, omission by design) so two of four examples now demonstrate omission and pattern-default toward firing is corrected. The CRITICAL clauses added to the bullet bodies (naming the assessment-side condition explicitly — "when direction_of_travel is anything other than 'single_snapshot'... OMIT this sentence regardless of input"; "when is_kathekon is true or false (not null), OMIT this sentence regardless of input") close the n=5 hard miss directly by naming the COMPOSITION CONTRACT semantics in line with the heuristic.

The clauses are belt-and-braces against the n=5 hard miss; they explicitly name the assessment-side semantic in addition to the input-condition heuristic. ## 2026-05-08 — D-RETURN-TO-M1-CP5-PRIME-PRIME-RUBRIC-REFRESH-2026-05-08

**Decision:** Sub-session return-to-M1-CP5-prime-prime (rubric refresh #3 against post-M1-CP5f parallel-run sample) completed. Founder reviewed the six ADR-004 §6.4 rubric dimensions across 6 post-M1-CP5f rows (5 standard-depth full-sandwich + 1 Tier 1 ELEMENT_FUSION fire from F7) plus per-row prose spot-check across the 5 non-Tier-1 rows against the cumulative seven Revisions + Refinement to Revision 3 + Revision 8 + Q6 STAGE DISCIPLINE rule + Q2 truncation defense + M1-CP5f's D1 + D2 (Amendment 4). Verdict: **39/40 prose micro-checks pass — improvement from RTM1-CP5-prime's 38/40 via hard-miss closure.** Founder elected **Branch A — Cutover** rather than Branch B (revise-again at M1-CP5g) or Branch C (architectural revisit). M1-CP6 cutover (Critical tier; full Critical Change Protocol; R10 announcement; deployment-configuration change activating new prose path on user-facing route) follows next session.

**Reasoning:** Per ADR-004 §10's checkpoint table, return-to-M1-CP5-prime-prime is the third cutover-readiness verdict for the M1 arc and the load-bearing decision for cutover. Five rubric dimensions returned clean signals supporting cutover (L1 latency 12,637 ms median 11,772 p95 15,376 — comparable to predecessor's 13,032; L3 latency 17,824 ms median 18,471 p95 19,908 — +2.6% creep within tolerance, below 20,000 ms watch threshold and 25,000 ms flag; sandwich/bundled ratio 27,491/52,150 = 52.7% — sandwich still ~half bundled latency; cost $0.0340/req cumulative $0.2038 — under R5 $0.05 baseline; cost cap exceedances 0 over $0.05 / 0 over $0.10 — preserved; Tier 1 fires EF×1 from F7 — firing as designed). The sixth dimension (proximity match 40% — sandwich differentiating into deliberate + reflexive + habitual three-way while bundled mode-collapses to deliberate only) reproduces the M1-CP5 first-pass + RTM1-CP5 + RTM1-CP5-prime architectural finding. Step 3 prose spot-check across 5 non-Tier-1 rows: 39/40 micro-checks pass (Rev 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7, 8 + Q6 all 5/5; Rev 5 single-snapshot disclaimer 4/5 — F4 soft miss persists). **Hard miss (n=5 RTM1-CP5-prime, F8 stable trajectory) closed:** rows 2 (F2, direction_of_travel = stable) and 6 (F8, direction_of_travel = stable) both correctly OMITTED the single-snapshot disclaimer. The CRITICAL clauses added at M1-CP5f are functioning as belt-and-braces — assessment-side hard gate respected; no over-omission observed (Q8 closed). **Soft miss (n=4 RTM1-CP5-prime, F4) persists** — same fixture, same shape, second observation: F4's input ("I have to send the contract back today...") lacks iterative temporal hook + assessment direction_of_travel = single_snapshot + disclaimer fired, violating Revision 5's input-condition heuristic. Founder accepted the soft miss as a known-limitation post-cutover watch item rather than scoping M1-CP5g (Branch B). Reasoning: hard miss was the COMPOSITION CONTRACT violation that M1-CP5f targeted and closed; soft miss is a permissible-but-unnecessary mid-prose sentence that does not contradict the assessment (the prose still produces a correct, action-oriented response); cost of one more iteration ~2 hours vs gain at polish-level only. Branch C declined: only ONE residual recurring (not 3+); convergence trajectory across M1-CP5b → M1-CP5d → M1-CP5e → M1-CP5f shows each fix landing first time; deeper architectural change not warranted.

## 2026-05-08 — D-M1-CP6-CUTOVER-2026-05-08

**Decision:** Sub-session M1-CP6 — cutover commit applied. The bundled-depth call to `runSageReason` was removed from `/api/reason`; the translation-sandwich engine (`runSandwich` orchestrator over Layer 1 → Layer 2 → Layer 3) is now the sole user-facing path. Response shape changed from bundled-depth single object (`{ result, meta }`) to `translation-sandwich-v1` (`{ version, extraction, assessment, prose, meta, disclaimer }`) per ADR-004 §2.1. Cutover deployed to Vercel production; four verifications passed. **Layer 3 module status moves from Verified → Live; M1 arc completes.** R10 announcement declined under 3c election; founder accepted the trade-off explicitly. Three design choices elected at session-open: 1C (deterministic minimal fallback for Layer 1/2 throws — no bundled fallback), 2A (parallel-run retired — `runParallelSandwich` deprecated, retained for offline/testing), 3A (Tier 1 force-clarification first-turn surfacing wired via `issueContinuationToken`).

**Reasoning:** Per ADR-004 §10's checkpoint table, M1-CP6 is the cutover commit row — Critical-tier deployment-config change activating new prose path on the user-facing route. Predecessor session (RTM1-CP5-prime-prime, `D-RETURN-TO-M1-CP5-PRIME-PRIME-RUBRIC-REFRESH-2026-05-08`) verified 39/40 prose micro-checks, closed the hard miss, accepted F4 soft miss as known limitation post-cutover, and authorised cutover via founder's Branch A election. This session executed the cutover under the full Critical Change Protocol per project instructions §0c-ii. Founder elected 3c (skip R10 announcement) on grounds that no external `/api/reason` consumers are known despite agent_card.json + llms.txt agent discovery surface — the trade-off was named, founder owns the call. Three design choices (1C + 2A + 3A) elected after AI surfaced architectural ambiguity in ADR-004 (§9.1 vs §10 CP6 reconciliation), parallel-run posture options (2.A vs 2.B), and Tier 1 surfacing scope (3A vs 3B). Founder also surfaced and deferred a 2F architectural arc — captured in §"Deferred decision" below per PR7. Diff applied: 2 files touched (~150 LOC net change); both files edited via Edit tool with founder reading the diff in chat before approval. Critical Change Protocol's six steps captured visibly in the conversation; founder approved with "approved" — explicit acknowledgment of the four named risks (R10 / external-consumer breaking change; R20a perimeter preservation; Layer 1 failure visibility change under 1C; TRANSLATION_SANDWICH_TIER1_SECRET env-var dependency for 3A).

**Critical Change Protocol record (per project instructions §0c-ii — six steps verbatim from session conversation):**

1. **What is changing — plain language.** /api/reason stops calling the bundled-depth engine entirely. It now calls only the translation-sandwich engine and returns the sandwich's response. From the user's point of view: when they hit /api/reason, they receive a response with a different structure — fields named `extraction`, `assessment`, and `prose` (each carrying the explicit Layer 1, Layer 2, and Layer 3 outputs) instead of the bundled-depth single object. The bundled engine code remains in `/website/src/lib/sage-reason-engine.ts` for other endpoints (M2/M3/M4 consumer migrations). Three new behaviors come with the cutover: Tier 1 force-clarification responses now reach the user (with a continuation token to re-submit after answering), Layer 1/2 failures return a polite minimal-shape fallback (no bundled call, no error to the user), and the response always carries the R3 disclaimer.

2. **What could break — specific worst case.** Four named risks: (a) external agent developers parsing /api/reason responses against the bundled-depth shape will see breaking change at cutover — their parsers fail on the new shape; per 3c election, no R10 announcement; if external consumers exist (the agent_card.json + llms.txt agent discovery surface implies some may), they get no warning. (b) The R20a perimeter at line 173 must remain inviolate (AC5 + PR6); the diff preserves it verbatim with `runSandwich` strictly AFTER `enforceDistressCheck`. (c) Layer 1 LLM failures during cutover — today silently absorbed by bundled fallback; post-cutover under 1C user sees a polite minimal-shape fallback (200 status, structured response with `fallback: true` in meta); behavior change, honest, not stranding the user, but visibly different. (d) TRANSLATION_SANDWICH_TIER1_SECRET env var must be set on Vercel for Tier 1 issuance to work; if unset and Tier 1 fires, user sees 503 with `continuation_token_engine_unavailable`; per predecessor production state, env var is already set in all three environments.

3. **What happens to existing sessions.** Auth/session unaffected. The cutover does not touch authentication, session cookies, JWT validation, or session-bridge state. The translation-sandwich path sits POST-perimeter (per ADR-004 §8); existing user sessions continue uninterrupted. On their next /api/reason call they receive the new response shape, but the session itself is preserved. AC7 NOT engaged.

4. **Rollback plan.** `git revert HEAD` of the cutover commit + `git push origin main`. Vercel rebuild restores the parallel-run flow as user-facing. Rollback is itself a breaking change going the other way for any external consumers who adopted the new schema during the cutover window — public "rollback in effect" notice required if rollback occurs even though no R10 announcement was made before cutover.

5. **Verification step (founder-performable, post-deploy).** Four verifications named: (1) Happy path, new shape — non-distressing fixture via /admin/test-reason; confirm version + extraction + assessment + prose + disclaimer present; NOT bundled-depth shape. (2) R20a perimeter still fires — distress-flagged input ("I've been thinking about ending my life..."); confirm `{ distress_detected: true, severity, redirect_message }`; engine NOT called. (3) Tier 1 surfacing — fixture F7 (ELEMENT_FUSION); confirm `clarification_required: true`, `intake_tier: 1`, non-empty `continuation_token`. (4) Vercel logs clean — no `[/api/reason]` errors around deploy timestamp.

6. **Explicit founder approval specific to named risks.** Founder responded "approved" — having read the four named risks in Step 2 (especially the R10 / external-consumer risk under 3c, and the Layer 1 failure visibility change under 1C). Approval was for THIS commit only, not blanket M1-arc approval.

**Files touched:**

- `/website/src/lib/translation-sandwich/parallel-run.ts` — added new export `runSandwich(params: SandwichInput): Promise<SandwichRunResult>` (production entry point; calls `runSandwichInner`; no DB I/O); marked `runParallelSandwich` deprecated via JSDoc (retained for offline/testing/curiosity per founder's 2F brainstorm note).
- `/website/src/app/api/reason/route.ts` — (a) imports updated: removed `runSageReason` runtime import, kept `type ReasonDepth` + added `EVALUATIVE_DISCLAIMER` from sage-reason-engine; replaced `runParallelSandwich` import with `runSandwich`; added `issueContinuationToken` + `Tier1SecretMissingError` from tier1-token. (b) Removed `void previousTrigger` placeholder (now load-bearing). (c) Replaced post-context-loading block: removed `bundledStartedAt` + `bundledPromise = runSageReason(...)` + `runParallelSandwich({...})` + `await bundledPromise` + `NextResponse.json(result)`; new flow = single `await runSandwich({...})` + three branches (Tier 1 surfacing → issue token + splice + return; Layer 1/2/3 throw → minimal fallback shape; happy path → disclaimer + previousTrigger meta integration + return). (d) Added `buildMinimalFallback(reason)` helper before OPTIONS export.
- `/operations/decision-log.md` — this entry appended (full form per project instructions §0c-ii Critical Change Protocol record).

**Election record — 3c (R10 declined):**

Founder elected 3c at session-open per Pre-conditions §3 of the M1-CP6 next-session prompt. Reasoning captured here per the deliberate-trade-off requirement: (a) no known external `/api/reason` consumers exist as of cutover; (b) sagereasoning.com itself is the primary known consumer and it deploys atomically with the route change; (c) the 14-day R10 announcement window would delay the cutover for diminishing return on a non-confirmed external consumer base; (d) the agent_card.json + llms.txt agent discovery surface implies external consumers may exist — if they do, they will see breaking schema change without warning at cutover; (e) founder explicitly accepted the trade-off after AI flagged it. **Mitigation if external consumers surface post-cutover:** publish a retroactive migration guide referencing the schema change; offer a courtesy delay window for migration.

**Deferred decision (per PR7 — Decisions Not Made Are Documented) — 2F architectural arc:**

**What was considered:** Founder proposed 2F at M1-CP6 session-open as an alternative to 2A/2B/2C parallel-run posture options. The proposal: after sandwich Layer 2 lands, surface three components — (A) numerical scores per Layer 2 step + final outcome (for agents, UI graphics, longitudinal proximity tracking); (B) current Sonnet Layer 3 prose (unchanged); (C) new Opus 4.7 LLM call taking raw input + raw user data + Layer 2 path/output, producing input-specific contextualizing prose in an integrative voice. Bundled engine repurposed as testing/curiosity-only path (which 2A retirement effectively achieves).

**Founder's underlying intent:** preserve bundled's input-specific responsiveness — the value bundled offers that sandwich Layer 3 (Sonnet) doesn't fully capture — by replacing bundled with a more capable LLM (Opus) that has explicit access to both the raw input AND the deterministic Layer 2 assessment. The deterministic Layer 2 stays stable (auditable, reviewable); a more capable LLM produces the input-specific responsive prose on top.

**Why deferred:** (a) 2F is a substantial architectural change — not a parallel-run posture decision but an engine-architecture change adding a new layer (Layer 4 or Layer 3b). (b) Larger than M1-CP6 was scoped for — would require multiple ADRs, cost projection work, parallel-run observation of the new Opus layer before user-facing ship. (c) Per PR1 single-endpoint discipline + ADR-004 §10 parallel-run-before-cutover pattern, 2F should be its own multi-session arc with parallel-run observation before cutover. (d) Shipping 2F today would have been the first time this project commits an LLM-layer architecture without observation data first.

**Four open clarifying questions (founder to answer at 2F arc session-open):**

(i) **Model.** "Opus 4.7" — AI's system prompt model list as of May 2026 names Claude Opus 4.6 as latest Opus. Founder to confirm: is 4.7 actually shipped, or is "4.7" shorthand for "the latest Opus available"?

(ii) **Replace or augment Layer 3?** Three sub-options: (1) Opus replaces Sonnet Layer 3 entirely — single prose output. (2) Opus runs alongside Sonnet Layer 3 — user sees both. (3) Layer 3 retired in favor of Opus producing both structured fields AND integrative voice.

(iii) **Component A — numerical scoring scope.** Per-mechanism quality scores (already exist as qualitative `assessment.stage_scores`)? Final proximity score? Both? Categorical-as-numeric (1–5 mapping to existing 5 levels) or true numeric (0–100)? Affects R6c reconciliation — R6c says "Qualitative proximity levels, not numeric 0–100" so true-numeric requires R6c amendment (Critical-tier governance change).

(iv) **Component C — "other raw text data stored from the user."** What's in scope? Practitioner_context + project_context (current four-layer)? Mentor profile? Journal entries? Recent interaction history? All of the above?

**Cost + latency considerations to surface at 2F arc session-open:**

- Per-request cost roughly tripling: current sandwich ~$0.034 → with Opus added ~$0.10–0.13. R5 ratio (paid-tier revenue must cover ≥2x LLM cost) compresses meaningfully. Deliberate-choice exercise per R0 + R5 needed.
- Latency: Opus calls run ~25–40s for prompts of this complexity; current Sonnet L3 ~17s. Sequential = 50–70s, near Vercel 60s timeout. Parallel Opus + Sonnet (max 25–40s) recommended over sequential.

**Governance work 2F arc would trigger:**

- ADR-009 (or ADR-004 amendment) — new Layer 4 / Layer 3b specification.
- AC1 amendment — add Opus row to model selection table.
- R6c reconciliation — depending on Component A scoring scope.
- R5 cost projection update.
- ADR-007 amendment or supersession — depending on whether Sonnet Layer 3 is retired.
- Verification harness extension — Opus output validation, R7 source fidelity, R8a/R8c discipline.
- New module: `layer4-opus-contextualization.ts` (or `layer3b-opus.ts`).

**Sequencing recommendation:** treat 2F as a separate architectural arc — call it "M1-Plus" or "Layer 3b Opus contextualization arc." Parallel-run observation of the Opus layer before user-facing ship.

**Cross-reference for 2F revisit:** this session's chat log contains the full discussion of 2F including the input-specificity concerns I raised about 2C and how 2F resolves them.

## 2026-05-09 — D-ITEM-I-viii-CONSUMER-PAGE-ADAPTATION-2026-05-09

**Decision:** Sub-session Item I (viii) — consumer-page-vs-API-shape adaptation following the M1-CP6 cutover (2026-05-08). Four reasoning-display surfaces adapted to the translation-sandwich-v1 response shape: (1) /mentor-hub mentor-reply path now reads `data.prose.philosophical_reflection` with safe fallbacks (was reading `data.result || data.response` and falling through to "I acknowledge your message." placeholder every turn); (2) /ops-hub Stoic Check display shows a structured prose.philosophical_reflection + improvement_guidance + summary three-paragraph layout plus qualitative `assessment.katorthoma_proximity` per R6c (was reading `reasoning` + numeric `proximity_rating`, falling through to JSON dump); (3) /ops-hub Alert evaluation display does minimum-extraction reading `prose.summary` with `philosophical_reflection` fallback (was reading `reasoning`, falling through to JSON dump); (4) /ops-hub Decision Scoring display polished per founder election — ranked option cards with proximity badge + kathekon line + stoic_insight paragraph + passions_detected chips, recommended option highlighted with green border + star, reasoning_receipt summary, disclaimer (was reading `comparison` + `recommendation`, falling through to JSON dump). All four surfaces additionally handle distress-detected (`{distress_detected: true, redirect_message}`) and Tier-1 force-clarification (`{clarification_required: true, clarification: {question_text}}`) auxiliary shapes explicitly. /private-mentor audited; no adaptation needed (the page's /api/reason call awaits-and-discards). Item I bundle: (i)+(ii)+(iii)+(vi)+(vii)+(viii) Closed; (iv)+(v) remain open.

**Reasoning:** Direct continuation of the post-M1-CP6 hygiene cleanup. The translation-sandwich-v1 cutover replaced the bundled-engine response shape on /api/reason with `{version, extraction, assessment, prose, meta, disclaimer}`; consumer pages still read the old `data.result || data.response` / `data.reasoning` / `data.comparison` fields. Pre-fix user impact: real reasoning prose was hidden behind placeholder text on /mentor-hub and behind raw JSON on /ops-hub for every reasoning-display surface. Founder-elected scope: bundle all four surfaces in one session (vs split); presentable polish on Decision Scoring; minimum extraction on the other three. The polish elaboration is justified by Decision Scoring being the highest-information-density surface (per-option ranking + structured fields + receipts) and the only surface where minimum extraction would still produce a hard-to-read display (multiple option records concatenated). Per /api/score-decision route handler reading, the buildEnvelope shape places the meaningful fields at `result.options_scored[]`, `result.recommended`, `result.reasoning_receipt`, `result.disclaimer`. Per /lib/translation-sandwich/parallel-run.ts (`runSandwich` happy-path return) and /lib/translation-sandwich/layer3-prose.ts (`Layer3Prose` interface), the user-facing prose fields on /api/reason are `prose.philosophical_reflection`, `prose.improvement_guidance`, `prose.summary`. The minimal-fallback shape (route lines 363-389) shares the same prose field names, so the adaptations work transparently across happy-path + minimal-fallback responses.
